EVATok: Adaptive Length Video Tokenization for Efficient Visual Autoregressive Generation
Authors: Tianwei Xiong, Jun Hao Liew, Zilong Huang, Zhijie Lin, Jiashi Feng, Xihui Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://silentview.github.io/EVATok/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive (AR) video generative models rely on video tokenizers that compress pixels into discrete token sequences. The length of these token sequences is crucial for balancing reconstruction quality against downstream generation computational cost. Traditional video tokenizers apply a uniform token assignment across temporal blocks of different videos, often wasting tokens on simple, static, or repetitive segments while underserving dynamic or complex ones. To address this inefficiency, we introduce $\textbf{EVATok}$, a framework to produce $\textbf{E}$fficient $\textbf{V}$ideo $\textbf{A}$daptive $\textbf{Tok}$enizers. Our framework estimates optimal token assignments for each video to achieve the best quality-cost trade-off, develops lightweight routers for fast prediction of these optimal assignments, and trains adaptive tokenizers that encode videos based on the assignments predicted by routers. We demonstrate that EVATok delivers substantial improvements in efficiency and overall quality for video reconstruction and downstream AR generation. Enhanced by our advanced training recipe that integrates video semantic encoders, EVATok achieves superior reconstruction and state-of-the-art class-to-video generation on UCF-101, with at least 24.4% savings in average token usage compared to the prior state-of-the-art LARP and our fixed-length baseline.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform tokenization in autoregressive video generation, which wastes tokens on static segments while underserving dynamic ones, EVATok introduces an adaptive tokenization framework that optimizes token allocation per video for quality-cost balance. It employs lightweight routers to predict optimal token assignments and trains adaptive tokenizers that encode videos according to these predictions. Experiments show EVATok achieves superior video reconstruction and state-of-the-art class-to-video generation on UCF-101, with at least 24.4% reduction in average token usage compared to prior methods.
为解决传统视频分词器在静态段落浪费令牌、动态段落不足的问题，EVATok提出一种自适应框架，为每个视频估计最优令牌分配以平衡重建质量与计算成本。该框架使用轻量级路由器快速预测分配方案，并训练自适应分词器根据路由器预测进行视频编码。实验表明，EVATok在UCF-101上实现更优重建效果和最先进的类别到视频生成能力，平均令牌使用量较前序最优方法LARP及固定长度基线减少至少24.4%。
MM-CondChain: A Programmatically Verified Benchmark for Visually Grounded Deep Compositional Reasoning
Authors: Haozhan Shen, Shilin Yan, Hongwei Xue, Shuaiqi Lu, Xiaojun Tang, Guannan Zhang, Tiancheng Zhao, Jianwei Yin
Venue: MM
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://accio-lab.github.io/MM-CondChain
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to carry out visual workflows such as navigating GUIs, where the next step depends on verified visual compositional conditions (e.g., "if a permission dialog appears and the color of the interface is green, click Allow") and the process may branch or terminate early. Yet this capability remains under-evaluated: existing benchmarks focus on shallow-compositions or independent-constraints rather than deeply chained compositional conditionals. In this paper, we introduce MM-CondChain, a benchmark for visually grounded deep compositional reasoning. Each benchmark instance is organized as a multi-layer reasoning chain, where every layer contains a non-trivial compositional condition grounded in visual evidence and built from multiple objects, attributes, or relations. To answer correctly, an MLLM must perceive the image in detail, reason over multiple visual elements at each step, and follow the resulting execution path to the final outcome. To scalably construct such workflow-style data, we propose an agentic synthesis pipeline: a Planner orchestrates layer-by-layer generation of compositional conditions, while a Verifiable Programmatic Intermediate Representation (VPIR) ensures each layer's condition is mechanically verifiable. A Composer then assembles these verified layers into complete instructions. Using this pipeline, we construct benchmarks across three visual domains: natural images, data charts, and GUI trajectories. Experiments on a range of MLLMs show that even the strongest model attains only 53.33 Path F1, with sharp drops on hard negatives and as depth or predicate complexity grows, confirming that deep compositional reasoning remains a fundamental challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MM-CondChain：视觉接地深度组合推理的程序验证基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正越来越多地用于执行视觉工作流，如导航图形用户界面，其中下一步依赖于经验证的视觉组合条件（例如，“如果出现权限对话框且界面颜色为绿色，则点击允许”），且流程可能分支或提前终止。然而，这一能力尚未得到充分评估：现有基准侧重于浅层组合或独立约束，而非深层链式组合条件。本文提出MM-CondChain，一个视觉接地深度组合推理的基准。每个基准实例组织为多层推理链，每层包含一个基于视觉证据、由多个对象、属性或关系构建的非平凡组合条件。要正确回答，MLLM必须细致感知图像，在每一步推理多个视觉元素，并遵循由此产生的执行路径至最终结果。为可扩展构建此类工作流式数据，我们提出一种代理合成管道：规划器逐层生成组合条件，可验证的程序化中间表示（VPIR）确保每层条件可机械验证；合成器再将这些验证过的层组装为完整指令。通过该管道，我们在三个视觉领域构建基准：自然图像、数据图表和GUI轨迹。在多种MLLM上的实验表明，即使最强模型也仅达到53.33路径F1，且在困难负例或深度/谓词复杂度增加时急剧下降，证实深度组合推理仍是一个根本性挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in visually grounded, deeply chained compositional reasoning tasks—such as navigating GUIs based on complex visual conditions—the authors introduce MM-CondChain, a benchmark designed to assess such reasoning through multi-layered, conditionally branching workflows. Each instance requires models to perceive visual evidence, reason over multiple objects or relations at each layer, and follow execution paths to final outcomes. To construct this benchmark, they employ an agentic synthesis pipeline: a Planner generates layered conditions, a Verifiable Programmatic Intermediate Representation (VPIR) ensures each condition is mechanically verifiable, and a Composer assembles them into executable instructions. Evaluated across natural images, charts, and GUI trajectories, experiments reveal that even the strongest MLLM achieves only 53.33 Path F1, with performance sharply declining on harder negatives and as chain depth or predicate complexity increases, underscoring the difficulty of deep compositional reasoning.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉引导下的深层组合推理能力——如根据复杂视觉条件导航GUI界面——作者提出了MM-CondChain基准，通过多层条件分支工作流评估此类推理。每个实例要求模型感知视觉证据、在每层推理多个对象或关系，并跟随执行路径得出最终结果。为构建该基准，他们采用代理合成流程：规划器逐层生成组合条件，可验证的程序化中间表示（VPIR）确保每层条件可机械验证，合成器将这些验证层组装为可执行指令。在自然图像、图表和GUI轨迹三个视觉领域评估后，实验表明即使最强模型也仅达到53.33路径F1，且在困难负样本、链深度或谓词复杂度增加时性能急剧下降，凸显深层组合推理仍是核心挑战。
OmniStream: Mastering Perception, Reconstruction and Action in Continuous Streams
Authors: Yibin Yan, Jilan Xu, Shangzhe Di, Haoning Wu, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Technical Report. Project Page: https://go2heart.github.io/omnistream/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern visual agents require representations that are general, causal, and physically structured to operate in real-time streaming environments. However, current vision foundation models remain fragmented, specializing narrowly in image semantic perception, offline temporal modeling, or spatial geometry. This paper introduces OmniStream, a unified streaming visual backbone that effectively perceives, reconstructs, and acts from diverse visual inputs. By incorporating causal spatiotemporal attention and 3D rotary positional embeddings (3D-RoPE), our model supports efficient, frame-by-frame online processing of video streams via a persistent KV-cache. We pre-train OmniStream using a synergistic multi-task framework coupling static and temporal representation learning, streaming geometric reconstruction, and vision-language alignment on 29 datasets. Extensive evaluations show that, even with a strictly frozen backbone, OmniStream achieves consistently competitive performance with specialized experts across image and video probing, streaming geometric reconstruction, complex video and spatial reasoning, as well as robotic manipulation (unseen at training). Rather than pursuing benchmark-specific dominance, our work demonstrates the viability of training a single, versatile vision backbone that generalizes across semantic, spatial, and temporal reasoning, i.e., a more meaningful step toward general-purpose visual understanding for interactive and embodied agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniStream：掌握连续流中的感知、重建与行动
现代视觉代理需要具备通用性、因果性和物理结构化的表征，以在实时流式环境中运行。然而，当前视觉基础模型仍存在碎片化问题，主要专注于图像语义感知、离线时序建模或空间几何。本文提出了OmniStream，一种统一的流式视觉骨干网络，可从多样化的视觉输入中有效感知、重建并执行动作。通过引入因果时空注意力机制和三维旋转位置编码（3D-RoPE），该模型支持通过持久KV缓存实现视频流的高效逐帧在线处理。我们采用协同多任务框架对OmniStream进行预训练，结合静态与时序表征学习、流式几何重建及视觉-语言对齐，在29个数据集上进行训练。大量评估表明，即使骨干网络完全冻结，OmniStream在图像与视频探查、流式几何重建、复杂视频与空间推理以及机器人操作（训练中未见）等任务中仍能持续保持与专用专家相当的竞争力。我们的工作不追求特定基准的主导地位，而是证明了训练单一、多功能视觉骨干网络以实现语义、空间与时间推理的泛化是可行的，即朝着交互式与具身代理的通用视觉理解迈出了更有意义的一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified visual understanding in real-time streaming environments, this paper proposes OmniStream, a single visual backbone that integrates perception, reconstruction, and action through causal spatiotemporal attention and 3D-RoPE embeddings, enabling frame-by-frame online processing with persistent KV-cache. Pre-trained on 29 datasets via a multi-task framework combining static semantics, temporal modeling, geometric reconstruction, and vision-language alignment, OmniStream achieves competitive performance even with a frozen backbone across diverse tasks including image/video probing, geometric reconstruction, spatial reasoning, and robotic manipulation—demonstrating strong generalization without task-specific tuning.
为实现实时流媒体环境中的统一视觉理解，本文提出OmniStream，通过因果时空注意力机制与3D-RoPE嵌入，支持帧级在线处理并利用持久KV缓存。该模型在29个数据集上通过结合语义、几何与视觉-语言学习的多任务框架进行预训练，即使骨干网络冻结，仍能在图像/视频探查、几何重建、空间推理及机器人操作等任务中保持竞争力，且部分任务在训练中未见过，展现出强大的泛化能力，无需任务特定优化。
GRADE: Benchmarking Discipline-Informed Reasoning in Image Editing
Authors: Mingxin Liu, Ziqian Fan, Zhaokai Wang, Leyao Gu, Zirun Zhu, Yiguo He, Yuchen Yang, Changyao Tian, Xiangyu Zhao, Ning Liao, Shaofeng Zhang, Qibing Ren, Zhihang Zhong, Xuanhe Zhou, Junchi Yan, Xue Yang
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: 49 pages, 23 figures, 10 tables; Project Page: https://grade-bench.github.io/, Code: https://github.com/VisionXLab/GRADE, Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/VisionXLab/GRADE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Unified multimodal models target joint understanding, reasoning, and generation, but current image editing benchmarks are largely confined to natural images and shallow commonsense reasoning, offering limited assessment of this capability under structured, domain-specific constraints. In this work, we introduce GRADE, the first benchmark to assess discipline-informed knowledge and reasoning in image editing. GRADE comprises 520 carefully curated samples across 10 academic domains, spanning from natural science to social science. To support rigorous evaluation, we propose a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that jointly assesses Discipline Reasoning, Visual Consistency, and Logical Readability. Extensive experiments on 20 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source models reveal substantial limitations in current models under implicit, knowledge-intensive editing settings, leading to large performance gaps. Beyond quantitative scores, we conduct rigorous analyses and ablations to expose model shortcomings and identify the constraints within disciplinary editing. Together, GRADE pinpoints key directions for the future development of unified multimodal models, advancing the research on discipline-informed image editing and reasoning. Our benchmark and evaluation code are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRADE：图像编辑中学科知识推理能力的基准评测
统一多模态模型旨在实现联合理解、推理与生成，但当前图像编辑基准主要局限于自然图像和浅层常识推理，难以评估其在结构化、领域特定约束下的能力。本文提出GRADE，这是首个评估图像编辑中学科知识与推理能力的基准。GRADE涵盖10个学术领域、520个精心筛选的样本，从自然科学到社会科学均有覆盖。为支持严谨评估，我们提出多维度评估协议，联合评估学科推理、视觉一致性与逻辑可读性。在20个当前最先进的开源与闭源模型上的大量实验表明，现有模型在隐含、知识密集型编辑场景下存在显著局限，导致性能差距巨大。除定量评分外，我们还进行深入分析与消融实验，揭示模型缺陷并识别学科编辑中的约束条件。GRADE为统一多模态模型的未来发展指明关键方向，推动学科导向图像编辑与推理研究。我们的基准与评估代码已公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate unified multimodal models under structured, domain-specific image editing tasks, this work introduces GRADE, the first benchmark assessing discipline-informed reasoning across 10 academic domains with 520 curated samples. It employs a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol measuring Discipline Reasoning, Visual Consistency, and Logical Readability. Experiments on 20 state-of-the-art models reveal significant performance gaps under implicit, knowledge-intensive editing scenarios, highlighting current models' limitations in integrating domain-specific knowledge. The benchmark also enables ablation studies that expose model weaknesses and constrain disciplinary editing, guiding future model development.
为评估统一多模态模型在结构化、领域特定的图像编辑任务中的表现，本研究提出GRADE基准，首次评估跨10个学术领域（含520个精心筛选样本）的学科知识推理能力。该基准采用多维评估协议，综合衡量学科推理、视觉一致性与逻辑可读性。在20个前沿模型上的实验表明，现有模型在隐含知识密集型编辑场景下表现显著不足，存在明显性能差距，揭示了模型在整合领域知识方面的局限。基准同时支持消融分析，明确模型缺陷与学科编辑约束，为未来模型发展提供方向指引。
$Ψ_0$: An Open Foundation Model Towards Universal Humanoid Loco-Manipulation
Authors: Songlin Wei, Hongyi Jing, Boqian Li, Zhenyu Zhao, Jiageng Mao, Zhenhao Ni, Sicheng He, Jie Liu, Xiawei Liu, Kaidi Kang, Sheng Zang, Weiduo Yuan, Marco Pavone, Di Huang, Yue Wang
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce $Ψ_0$ (Psi-Zero), an open foundation model to address challenging humanoid loco-manipulation tasks. While existing approaches often attempt to address this fundamental problem by co-training on large and diverse human and humanoid data, we argue that this strategy is suboptimal due to the fundamental kinematic and motion disparities between humans and humanoid robots. Therefore, data efficiency and model performance remain unsatisfactory despite the considerable data volume. To address this challenge, \ours\;decouples the learning process to maximize the utility of heterogeneous data sources. Specifically, we propose a staged training paradigm with different learning objectives: First, we autoregressively pre-train a VLM backbone on large-scale egocentric human videos to acquire generalizable visual-action representations. Then, we post-train a flow-based action expert on high-quality humanoid robot data to learn precise robot joint control. Our research further identifies a critical yet often overlooked data recipe: in contrast to approaches that scale with noisy Internet clips or heterogeneous cross-embodiment robot datasets, we demonstrate that pre-training on high-quality egocentric human manipulation data followed by post-training on domain-specific real-world humanoid trajectories yields superior performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that \ours\ achieves the best performance using only about 800 hours of human video data and 30 hours of real-world robot data, outperforming baselines pre-trained on more than 10$\times$ as much data by over 40\% in overall success rate across multiple tasks. We will open-source the entire ecosystem to the community, including a data processing and training pipeline, a humanoid foundation model, and a real-time action inference engine.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of co-training human and humanoid data due to fundamental kinematic disparities, the authors propose $Ψ_0$, a staged foundation model that decouples learning into two phases: first, autoregressive pre-training of a vision-language model on high-quality egocentric human videos to capture general visual-action representations; second, post-training of a flow-based action expert on real-world humanoid robot data to refine precise joint control. Experimental results show that this approach achieves superior performance using only 800 hours of human video and 30 hours of robot data, outperforming baselines trained on over 10 times more data by more than 40% in overall success rate across multiple tasks.
为解决人类与人形机器人运动学差异导致的共训数据效率低下问题，作者提出$Ψ_0$分阶段训练框架：先在大规模第一人称人类视频上预训练视觉-语言模型以获取通用视觉-动作表征，再在高质量人形机器人数据上微调基于流的动作专家以实现精准关节控制。实验表明，该方法仅用800小时人类视频和30小时机器人数据，即在多项任务中以超40%的成功率超越使用十倍数据量基线模型，验证了其更高的数据效率与性能表现。
Video Streaming Thinking: VideoLLMs Can Watch and Think Simultaneously
Authors: Yiran Guan, Liang Yin, Dingkang Liang, Jianzhong Ju, Zhenbo Luo, Jian Luan, Yuliang Liu, Xiang Bai
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Online Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) play a critical role in supporting responsive, real-time interaction. Existing methods focus on streaming perception, lacking a synchronized logical reasoning stream. However, directly applying test-time scaling methods incurs unacceptable response latency. To address this trade-off, we propose Video Streaming Thinking (VST), a novel paradigm for streaming video understanding. It supports a thinking while watching mechanism, which activates reasoning over incoming video clips during streaming. This design improves timely comprehension and coherent cognition while preserving real-time responsiveness by amortizing LLM reasoning latency over video playback. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive post-training pipeline that integrates VST-SFT, which structurally adapts the offline VideoLLM to causal streaming reasoning, and VST-RL, which provides end-to-end improvement through self-exploration in a multi-turn video interaction environment. Additionally, we devise an automated training-data synthesis pipeline that uses video knowledge graphs to generate high-quality streaming QA pairs, with an entity-relation grounded streaming Chain-of-Thought to enforce multi-evidence reasoning and sustained attention to the video stream. Extensive evaluations show that VST-7B performs strongly on online benchmarks, e.g. 79.5% on StreamingBench and 59.3% on OVO-Bench. Meanwhile, VST remains competitive on offline long-form or reasoning benchmarks. Compared with Video-R1, VST responds 15.7 times faster and achieves +5.4% improvement on VideoHolmes, demonstrating higher efficiency and strong generalization across diverse video understanding tasks. Code, data, and models will be released at https://github.com/1ranGuan/VST.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视频流式思考：VideoLLMs可边观看边思考
在线视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）在支持响应式、实时交互中起关键作用。现有方法聚焦流式感知，缺乏同步的逻辑推理流。然而直接应用测试时扩展方法会导致不可接受的响应延迟。为解决此权衡，我们提出视频流式思考（VST），一种新颖的流式视频理解范式。它支持边观看边思考机制，在流式播放过程中对传入视频片段激活推理。该设计通过将LLM推理延迟摊销至视频播放中，提升及时理解与连贯认知，同时保持实时响应性。此外，我们引入全面的后训练流程，整合VST-SFT（结构化适配离线VideoLLM至因果流式推理）与VST-RL（通过多轮视频交互环境中的自探索实现端到端优化）。同时，我们设计自动化训练数据合成流程，利用视频知识图谱生成高质量流式问答对，并通过实体-关系锚定的流式思维链强制多证据推理与持续关注视频流。大量评估表明，VST-7B在在线基准上表现强劲，如在StreamingBench上达79.5%，在OVO-Bench上达59.3%。同时，VST在离线长视频或推理基准上仍具竞争力。相比Video-R1，VST响应速度快15.7倍，并在VideoHolmes上提升5.4%，展现出更高效率与对多样化视频理解任务的强泛化能力。代码、数据与模型将在https://github.com/1ranGuan/VST发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time video understanding with concurrent reasoning, the paper introduces Video Streaming Thinking (VST), a novel paradigm enabling VideoLLMs to process video clips while simultaneously performing logical reasoning. It achieves this by amortizing LLM inference latency over video playback, preserving responsiveness without sacrificing comprehension. The method combines VST-SFT for causal streaming adaptation and VST-RL for multi-turn interaction improvement, supported by an automated data pipeline generating entity-relation grounded Chain-of-Thought QA pairs. Experiments show VST-7B achieves 79.5% on StreamingBench and 59.3% on OVO-Bench, responds 15.7x faster than Video-R1, and gains +5.4% on VideoHolmes, demonstrating strong efficiency and generalization across streaming and offline benchmarks.
为实现视频流式处理中的实时推理，本文提出Video Streaming Thinking（VST）范式，使视频大模型在观看视频的同时同步进行逻辑推理，通过将LLM推理延迟摊薄至视频播放过程中，保持响应速度的同时提升理解能力。方法包含VST-SFT用于因果流式适配、VST-RL用于多轮交互优化，并结合自动化数据生成流水线，利用视频知识图谱构建实体关系驱动的思维链问答对。实验表明，VST-7B在StreamingBench上达79.5%，OVO-Bench上达59.3%，相较Video-R1响应快15.7倍，VideoHolmes提升5.4%，在流式与离线任务中均表现优异。
The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos
Authors: Mateusz Pach, Jessica Bader, Quentin Bouniot, Serge Belongie, Zeynep Akata
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜在颜色子空间：高维混沌中的涌现秩序
文本到图像生成模型发展迅速，但对生成图像实现精细控制仍具挑战，主要由于对语义信息编码机制理解有限。我们对FLUX.1 [Dev]变分自编码器潜在空间中的颜色表示提出解释，揭示其反映色相、饱和度与明度的结构。我们通过验证潜在颜色子空间（LCS）可预测并显式控制颜色，提出一种基于闭式潜在空间操作的完全无需训练的方法。代码见https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS。

Motivated by the lack of fine-grained control in text-to-image models due to unclear semantic encoding, this work investigates the latent space of FLUX.1 and identifies a structured color representation aligned with Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. HumDex:Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation Made Easy
Authors: Liang Heng, Yihe Tang, Jiajun Xu, Henghui Bao, Di Huang, Yue Wang
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck. Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

Motivated by the challenge of efficiently gathering high-quality demonstration data for humanoid dexterous manipulation, this work introduces HumDex, a portable teleoperation system that uses IMU-based motion tracking to overcome limitations of existing systems in portability and precision. It employs a learning-based retargeting method to generate natural hand motions without manual tuning, and enables efficient human motion data collection. 为解决人形机器人灵巧操作中高质量示范数据获取困难及现有遥操作系统在便携性与精度上的局限，本文提出HumDex系统，采用基于IMU的姿态追踪技术，在保证高精度全身体态捕捉的同时实现易部署。针对灵巧手控制，系统引入基于学习的重定向方法，无需人工调参即可生成自然流畅的手部动作。该系统还支持高效采集人类运动数据，进而构建两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类动作数据上预训练以学习通用先验，再在机器人数据上微调以弥合机体差异。实验表明，该方法显著提升机器人对新物体、新配置和新环境的泛化能力，且数据采集成本极低。
DreamVideo-Omni: Omni-Motion Controlled Multi-Subject Video Customization with Latent Identity Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yujie Wei, Xinyu Liu, Shiwei Zhang, Hangjie Yuan, Jinbo Xing, Zhekai Chen, Xiang Wang, Haonan Qiu, Rui Zhao, Yutong Feng, Ruihang Chu, Yingya Zhang, Yike Guo, Xihui Liu, Hongming Shan
First: 2026-03-12T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:12+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://dreamvideo-omni.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While large-scale diffusion models have revolutionized video synthesis, achieving precise control over both multi-subject identity and multi-granularity motion remains a significant challenge. Recent attempts to bridge this gap often suffer from limited motion granularity, control ambiguity, and identity degradation, leading to suboptimal performance on identity preservation and motion control. In this work, we present DreamVideo-Omni, a unified framework enabling harmonious multi-subject customization with omni-motion control via a progressive two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, we integrate comprehensive control signals for joint training, encompassing subject appearances, global motion, local dynamics, and camera movements. To ensure robust and precise controllability, we introduce a condition-aware 3D rotary positional embedding to coordinate heterogeneous inputs and a hierarchical motion injection strategy to enhance global motion guidance. Furthermore, to resolve multi-subject ambiguity, we introduce group and role embeddings to explicitly anchor motion signals to specific identities, effectively disentangling complex scenes into independent controllable instances. In the second stage, to mitigate identity degradation, we design a latent identity reward feedback learning paradigm by training a latent identity reward model upon a pretrained video diffusion backbone. This provides motion-aware identity rewards in the latent space, prioritizing identity preservation aligned with human preferences. Supported by our curated large-scale dataset and the comprehensive DreamOmni Bench for multi-subject and omni-motion control evaluation, DreamVideo-Omni demonstrates superior performance in generating high-quality videos with precise controllability.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of simultaneously controlling multi-subject identity and fine-grained motion in video synthesis, DreamVideo-Omni introduces a two-stage framework that first integrates diverse control signals—subject appearance, global motion, local dynamics, and camera movement—using a condition-aware 3D rotary positional embedding and hierarchical motion injection to enhance coordination and precision. To resolve identity ambiguity across subjects, it employs group and role embeddings to bind motion signals to specific identities. In the second stage, it leverages a latent identity reward model trained on a pretrained diffusion backbone to reinforce identity preservation during motion generation. Evaluated on a curated large-scale dataset and the DreamOmni Bench, the method achieves superior control fidelity and identity preservation compared to existing approaches.
为解决视频合成中多主体身份与多粒度运动同步控制的难题，DreamVideo-Omni 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段整合主体外观、全局运动、局部动态与摄像机运动等控制信号，通过条件感知3D旋转位置嵌入与分层运动注入实现精准协同；为消除身份混淆，引入组与角色嵌入将运动绑定至特定主体。第二阶段训练潜空间身份奖励模型，强化生成过程中的身份保持。在DreamOmni Bench评测中，该方法在保持主体身份的同时实现更精细的运动控制，显著优于现有方法。
NeuralOS: Towards Simulating Operating Systems via Neural Generative Models
Authors: Luke Rivard, Sun Sun, Hongyu Guo, Wenhu Chen, Yuntian Deng
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-07-11T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:59:08+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce NeuralOS, a neural framework that simulates graphical user interfaces (GUIs) of operating systems by directly predicting screen frames in response to user inputs such as mouse movements, clicks, and keyboard events. NeuralOS combines a recurrent neural network (RNN), which tracks computer state, with a diffusion-based neural renderer that generates screen images. The model is trained on a dataset of Ubuntu XFCE recordings, which include both randomly generated interactions and realistic interactions produced by AI agents. Experiments show that NeuralOS successfully renders realistic GUI sequences, accurately captures mouse interactions, and reliably predicts state transitions like application launches. Beyond reproducing existing systems, NeuralOS shows that synthesized training data can teach the model to simulate applications that were never installed, as illustrated by a Doom application, and suggests a path toward learning user interfaces purely from synthetic demonstrations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuralOS：通过神经生成模型模拟操作系统
我们提出了NeuralOS，一种神经框架，通过直接预测屏幕帧来模拟操作系统的图形用户界面（GUI），响应鼠标移动、点击和键盘事件等用户输入。NeuralOS结合了用于跟踪计算机状态的循环神经网络（RNN）与基于扩散的神经渲染器以生成屏幕图像。该模型在包含随机生成交互和由AI代理生成的现实交互的Ubuntu XFCE录制数据集上进行训练。实验表明，NeuralOS成功渲染出逼真的GUI序列，准确捕捉鼠标交互，并可靠预测应用启动等状态转换。除了复现现有系统，NeuralOS还表明合成训练数据可使模型模拟从未安装的应用程序（如Doom），并为仅从合成演示中学习用户界面提供了路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate operating system GUIs without relying on real hardware or installed software, NeuralOS employs a neural architecture combining an RNN for state tracking and a diffusion-based renderer for image generation. Trained on Ubuntu XFCE recordings—including both random and AI-generated interactions—the model learns to predict screen frames in response to user inputs. Experimental results demonstrate its ability to render realistic GUI sequences, accurately track mouse actions, and predict application launches, even simulating applications like Doom that were never installed, showcasing its potential to learn interfaces from synthetic demonstrations alone.
为实现无需真实硬件或安装软件即可模拟操作系统的目标，NeuralOS采用神经网络框架，通过循环神经网络追踪系统状态、扩散模型生成画面，直接根据鼠标、键盘等用户输入预测屏幕帧。该模型在包含随机与AI生成交互的Ubuntu XFCE数据集上训练，可生成逼真的GUI序列、准确捕捉鼠标操作并预测应用启动。更重要的是，它能模拟训练时未安装的应用（如Doom），表明合成数据可支持学习全新界面，无需实际接触。
Spatial-TTT: Streaming Visual-based Spatial Intelligence with Test-Time Training
Authors: Fangfu Liu, Diankun Wu, Jiawei Chi, Yimo Cai, Yi-Hsin Hung, Xumin Yu, Hao Li, Han Hu, Yongming Rao, Yueqi Duan
First: 2026-03-12T17:58:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:58:58+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://liuff19.github.io/Spatial-TTT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humans perceive and understand real-world spaces through a stream of visual observations. Therefore, the ability to streamingly maintain and update spatial evidence from potentially unbounded video streams is essential for spatial intelligence. The core challenge is not simply longer context windows but how spatial information is selected, organized, and retained over time. In this paper, we propose Spatial-TTT towards streaming visual-based spatial intelligence with test-time training (TTT), which adapts a subset of parameters (fast weights) to capture and organize spatial evidence over long-horizon scene videos. Specifically, we design a hybrid architecture and adopt large-chunk updates parallel with sliding-window attention for efficient spatial video processing. To further promote spatial awareness, we introduce a spatial-predictive mechanism applied to TTT layers with 3D spatiotemporal convolution, which encourages the model to capture geometric correspondence and temporal continuity across frames. Beyond architecture design, we construct a dataset with dense 3D spatial descriptions, which guides the model to update its fast weights to memorize and organize global 3D spatial signals in a structured manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Spatial-TTT improves long-horizon spatial understanding and achieves state-of-the-art performance on video spatial benchmarks. Project page: https://liuff19.github.io/Spatial-TTT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Spatial-TTT：基于视觉流的时空智能与测试时训练

Motivated by the need for models to continuously process and retain spatial information from unbounded video streams, this paper introduces Spatial-TTT, a test-time training framework that adapts fast weights to dynamically capture and organize spatial evidence over long-horizon scenes. The method combines a hybrid architecture with large-chunk updates and sliding-window attention for efficient video processing, while a spatial-predictive mechanism using 3D spatiotemporal convolutions enhances geometric and temporal consistency. Evaluated on video spatial benchmarks, Spatial-TTT outperforms existing methods by improving long-term spatial understanding and achieving state-of-the-art results through structured memorization of global 3D spatial signals.
为解决模型在无界视频流中持续感知与记忆空间信息的问题，本文提出Spatial-TTT框架，通过测试时训练动态调整快权重以捕捉和组织长时程场景中的空间证据。该方法结合混合架构、大块更新与滑动窗口注意力提升视频处理效率，并引入3D时空卷积的空间预测机制增强几何与时间连续性。在视频空间基准测试中，Spatial-TTT显著提升长时程空间理解能力，实现当前最优性能，通过结构化方式记忆全局3D空间信号。
Attend Before Attention: Efficient and Scalable Video Understanding via Autoregressive Gazing
Authors: Baifeng Shi, Stephanie Fu, Long Lian, Hanrong Ye, David Eigen, Aaron Reite, Boyi Li, Jan Kautz, Song Han, David M. Chan, Pavlo Molchanov, Trevor Darrell, Hongxu Yin
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:58:52+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project page: https://autogaze.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced general-purpose video understanding but struggle with long, high-resolution videos -- they process every pixel equally in their vision transformers (ViTs) or LLMs despite significant spatiotemporal redundancy. We introduce AutoGaze, a lightweight module that removes redundant patches before processed by a ViT or an MLLM. Trained with next-token prediction and reinforcement learning, AutoGaze autoregressively selects a minimal set of multi-scale patches that can reconstruct the video within a user-specified error threshold, eliminating redundancy while preserving information. Empirically, AutoGaze reduces visual tokens by 4x-100x and accelerates ViTs and MLLMs by up to 19x, enabling scaling MLLMs to 1K-frame 4K-resolution videos and achieving superior results on video benchmarks (e.g., 67.0% on VideoMME). Furthermore, we introduce HLVid: the first high-resolution, long-form video QA benchmark with 5-minute 4K-resolution videos, where an MLLM scaled with AutoGaze improves over the baseline by 10.1% and outperforms the previous best MLLM by 4.5%. Project page: https://autogaze.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先看后注意：通过自回归注视实现高效可扩展的视频理解
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽提升了通用视频理解能力，但在处理长时高分辨率视频时仍显吃力——其视觉Transformer（ViTs）或LLM会同等处理每个像素，忽视显著的空间时间冗余。我们提出AutoGaze，一个轻量模块，可在ViT或MLLM处理前剔除冗余块。该模块通过下一token预测与强化学习训练，自回归选择最小多尺度块集，以在用户指定误差阈值内重建视频，既消除冗余又保留信息。实验证明，AutoGaze可减少视觉token 4至100倍，加速ViT与MLLM最高达19倍，使MLLM可扩展至1000帧4K分辨率视频，并在视频基准测试中表现更优（如VideoMME达67.0%）。此外，我们推出HLVid：首个高分辨率长视频问答基准，包含5分钟4K视频，其中经AutoGaze扩展的MLLM相较基线提升10.1%，超越此前最佳MLLM 4.5%。项目页面：https://autogaze.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) in handling long, high-resolution videos due to uniform pixel processing, the authors propose AutoGaze, a lightweight autoregressive module that selectively samples minimal, multi-scale video patches while preserving content fidelity within a user-defined error threshold. Trained via next-token prediction and reinforcement learning, AutoGaze eliminates redundant visual tokens before feeding them into vision transformers or MLLMs, leading to a 4x–100x reduction in visual tokens and up to 19x acceleration. Experiments demonstrate that AutoGaze enables MLLMs to scale to 1K-frame 4K videos and achieve 67.0% on VideoMME, while also improving performance on the new HLVid benchmark by 10.1% over baseline and 4.5% over prior state-of-the-art.
为解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理长时高分辨率视频时因均匀关注每个像素导致的效率低下问题，作者提出AutoGaze，一个轻量级自回归模块，通过在视觉Transformer或MLLM前选择性采样最小多尺度视频块，同时在用户设定的误差阈值内保留内容。该模块通过下一个token预测与强化学习训练，有效去除冗余视觉token。实验表明，AutoGaze可将视觉token减少4至100倍，推理加速最高达19倍，使MLLMs可扩展至1K帧4K分辨率视频，并在VideoMME等基准上表现更优（67.0%）。作者还引入了HLVid，首个4K、5分钟长视频问答基准，其中增强版MLLM相比基线提升10.1%，优于此前最优模型4.5%。
EndoCoT: Scaling Endogenous Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Diffusion Models
Authors: Xuanlang Dai, Yujie Zhou, Long Xing, Jiazi Bu, Xilin Wei, Yuhong Liu, Beichen Zhang, Kai Chen, Yuhang Zang
First: 2026-03-12T17:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:58:48+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 18 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely integrated into diffusion frameworks primarily as text encoders to tackle complex tasks such as spatial reasoning. However, this paradigm suffers from two critical limitations: (i) MLLMs text encoder exhibits insufficient reasoning depth. Single-step encoding fails to activate the Chain-of-Thought process, which is essential for MLLMs to provide accurate guidance for complex tasks. (ii) The guidance remains invariant during the decoding process. Invariant guidance during decoding prevents DiT from progressively decomposing complex instructions into actionable denoising steps, even with correct MLLM encodings. To this end, we propose Endogenous Chain-of-Thought (EndoCoT), a novel framework that first activates MLLMs' reasoning potential by iteratively refining latent thought states through an iterative thought guidance module, and then bridges these states to the DiT's denoising process. Second, a terminal thought grounding module is applied to ensure the reasoning trajectory remains grounded in textual supervision by aligning the final state with ground-truth answers. With these two components, the MLLM text encoder delivers meticulously reasoned guidance, enabling the DiT to execute it progressively and ultimately solve complex tasks in a step-by-step manner. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks (e.g., Maze, TSP, VSP, and Sudoku) achieve an average accuracy of 92.1%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.3 percentage points.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EndoCoT：在扩散模型中扩展内生思维链推理
近期，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）被广泛集成到扩散框架中，主要作为文本编码器以应对空间推理等复杂任务。然而，该范式存在两个关键局限：(i) MLLMs的文本编码器推理深度不足。单步编码无法激活思维链过程，这是MLLMs为复杂任务提供准确指导所必需的。(ii) 解码过程中指导保持不变。解码时不变的指导阻碍了DiT将复杂指令逐步分解为可执行的去噪步骤，即使MLLM编码正确。为此，我们提出内生思维链（EndoCoT）框架：首先，通过迭代思维引导模块迭代优化潜在思维状态，激活MLLMs的推理潜力；其次，将这些状态与DiT的去噪过程衔接。此外，应用终端思维锚定模块，通过将最终状态与真实答案对齐，确保推理轨迹始终受文本监督。借助这两个组件，MLLM文本编码器提供精心推理的指导，使DiT能够逐步执行并最终以分步方式解决复杂任务。在多个基准（如迷宫、TSP、VSP和数独）上的广泛评估中，平均准确率达92.1%，优于最强基线8.3个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of MLLMs as text encoders in diffusion models—namely shallow reasoning and invariant guidance—the paper introduces EndoCoT, a framework that activates endogenous chain-of-thought reasoning by iteratively refining latent thought states via an iterative thought guidance module and grounding the final reasoning state with a terminal thought grounding module aligned to ground-truth answers. This enables the diffusion model to progressively decompose complex tasks into actionable denoising steps. Experimental results across benchmarks including Maze, TSP, VSP, and Sudoku show EndoCoT achieves an average accuracy of 92.1%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.3 percentage points.
为了解决多模态大语言模型作为扩散模型文本编码器时存在的推理深度不足和指导不变性问题，本文提出EndoCoT框架。该框架首先通过迭代思维引导模块逐步优化潜在思维状态，再通过终端思维锚定模块将最终推理结果与真实答案对齐，从而实现推理过程的逐步引导。在Maze、TSP、VSP和数独等多个基准测试中，EndoCoT平均准确率达到92.1%，比最强基线高出8.3个百分点。
DVD: Deterministic Video Depth Estimation with Generative Priors
Authors: Hongfei Zhang, Harold Haodong Chen, Chenfei Liao, Jing He, Zixin Zhang, Haodong Li, Yihao Liang, Kanghao Chen, Bin Ren, Xu Zheng, Shuai Yang, Kun Zhou, Yinchuan Li, Nicu Sebe, Ying-Cong Chen
First: 2026-03-12T17:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:58:06+00:00
Comments: Project: https://dvd-project.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing video depth estimation faces a fundamental trade-off: generative models suffer from stochastic geometric hallucinations and scale drift, while discriminative models demand massive labeled datasets to resolve semantic ambiguities. To break this impasse, we present DVD, the first framework to deterministically adapt pre-trained video diffusion models into single-pass depth regressors. Specifically, DVD features three core designs: (i) repurposing the diffusion timestep as a structural anchor to balance global stability with high-frequency details; (ii) latent manifold rectification (LMR) to mitigate regression-induced over-smoothing, enforcing differential constraints to restore sharp boundaries and coherent motion; and (iii) global affine coherence, an inherent property bounding inter-window divergence, which enables seamless long-video inference without requiring complex temporal alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DVD achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance across benchmarks. Furthermore, DVD successfully unlocks the profound geometric priors implicit in video foundation models using 163x less task-specific data than leading baselines. Notably, we fully release our pipeline, providing the whole training suite for SOTA video depth estimation to benefit the open-source community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DVD：基于生成先验的确定性视频深度估计
现有视频深度估计面临根本性权衡：生成模型易受随机几何幻觉和尺度漂移影响，而判别模型则需海量标注数据以解决语义歧义。为突破此瓶颈，我们提出DVD，首个将预训练视频扩散模型确定性适配为单次通过深度回归器的框架。具体而言，DVD包含三项核心设计：(i) 重用扩散时间步作为结构锚点，平衡全局稳定性与高频细节；(ii) 潜空间流形校正（LMR），缓解回归导致的过度平滑，通过微分约束恢复锐利边界与连贯运动；(iii) 全局仿射一致性，一种约束窗口间发散性的固有属性，支持无需复杂时序对齐的无缝长视频推理。大量实验表明，DVD在多个基准上实现零样本最优性能。此外，DVD仅用领先基线163倍更少的任务特定数据，便成功释放视频基础模型中蕴含的深层几何先验。值得注意的是，我们完全开源了整个训练流程，为开源社区提供SOTA视频深度估计工具链。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between generative models' geometric hallucinations and discriminative models' reliance on massive labeled data, DVD introduces a deterministic framework that adapts pre-trained video diffusion models into single-pass depth regressors. It employs three key innovations: using diffusion timesteps as structural anchors for stability and detail preservation, applying latent manifold rectification to counter over-smoothing and restore sharp boundaries, and leveraging global affine coherence to enable long-video inference without temporal alignment. Experimental results show DVD achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance and requires 163x less task-specific data than leading methods, while being fully open-sourced.
为解决生成模型几何伪影与判别模型数据依赖之间的矛盾，DVD提出首个将预训练视频扩散模型转化为单次推理深度回归器的确定性框架。其核心包含三部分：利用扩散时间步作为结构锚点平衡全局稳定与高频细节，通过潜在流形校正抑制过平滑并恢复边界与运动一致性，利用全局仿射一致性实现无需时序对齐的长视频推理。实验表明，DVD在多个基准上达到零样本最优性能，且仅需领先方法163倍更少的特定任务数据。
SciMDR: Benchmarking and Advancing Scientific Multimodal Document Reasoning
Authors: Ziyu Chen, Yilun Zhao, Chengye Wang, Rilyn Han, Manasi Patwardhan, Arman Cohan
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constructing scientific multimodal document reasoning datasets for foundation model training involves an inherent trade-off among scale, faithfulness, and realism. To address this challenge, we introduce the synthesize-and-reground framework, a two-stage pipeline comprising: (1) Claim-Centric QA Synthesis, which generates faithful, isolated QA pairs and reasoning on focused segments, and (2) Document-Scale Regrounding, which programmatically re-embeds these pairs into full-document tasks to ensure realistic complexity. Using this framework, we construct SciMDR, a large-scale training dataset for cross-modal comprehension, comprising 300K QA pairs with explicit reasoning chains across 20K scientific papers. We further construct SciMDR-Eval, an expert-annotated benchmark to evaluate multimodal comprehension within full-length scientific workflows. Experiments demonstrate that models fine-tuned on SciMDR achieve significant improvements across multiple scientific QA benchmarks, particularly in those tasks requiring complex document-level reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SciMDR：科学多模态文档推理的基准与推进

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of balancing scale, faithfulness, and realism in scientific multimodal document reasoning datasets, the authors propose a two-stage framework: Claim-Centric QA Synthesis generates accurate, focused QA pairs with reasoning chains, followed by Document-Scale Regrounding that embeds these into full-document contexts to simulate real-world complexity. Using this approach, they build SciMDR—a dataset of 300K QA pairs across 20K scientific papers—and SciMDR-Eval, an expert-annotated benchmark. Experimental results show that models fine-tuned on SciMDR significantly outperform baselines across multiple scientific QA benchmarks, especially in tasks demanding document-level reasoning.
为解决科学多模态文档推理数据集中规模、忠实度与真实感之间的权衡问题，作者提出两阶段框架：第一阶段为以主张为中心的问答合成，生成准确且聚焦的问答对及推理链；第二阶段为文档级重嵌入，将这些问答对程序化地嵌入完整文档任务中以模拟真实复杂度。基于此框架，他们构建了包含30万对问答、覆盖2万篇科学论文的SciMDR训练数据集及专家标注的SciMDR-Eval评估基准。实验表明，使用SciMDR微调的模型在多个科学问答基准上显著优于基线模型，尤其在需要文档级推理的任务中表现突出。
Matching Features, Not Tokens: Energy-Based Fine-Tuning of Language Models
Authors: Samy Jelassi, Mujin Kwun, Rosie Zhao, Yuanzhi Li, Nicolo Fusi, Yilun Du, Sham M. Kakade, Carles Domingo-Enrich
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-entropy (CE) training provides dense and scalable supervision for language models, but it optimizes next-token prediction under teacher forcing rather than sequence-level behavior under model rollouts. We introduce a feature-matching objective for language-model fine-tuning that targets sequence-level statistics of the completion distribution, providing dense semantic feedback without requiring a task-specific verifier or preference model. To optimize this objective efficiently, we propose energy-based fine-tuning (EBFT), which uses strided block-parallel sampling to generate multiple rollouts from nested prefixes concurrently, batches feature extraction over these rollouts, and uses the resulting embeddings to perform an on-policy policy-gradient update. We present a theoretical perspective connecting EBFT to KL-regularized feature-matching and energy-based modeling. Empirically, across Q&A coding, unstructured coding, and translation, EBFT matches RLVR and outperforms SFT on downstream accuracy while achieving a lower validation cross-entropy than both methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of cross-entropy training in capturing sequence-level behavior, this paper introduces a feature-matching objective for fine-tuning language models that targets completion distribution statistics without requiring task-specific verifiers. To enable efficient optimization, it proposes energy-based fine-tuning (EBFT), which concurrently samples multiple rollouts from nested prefixes, batches feature extraction across them, and performs on-policy policy gradient updates using the resulting embeddings. Experiments show EBFT matches the performance of RLVR and surpasses supervised fine-tuning on downstream tasks while achieving lower validation cross-entropy than both methods.
为克服交叉熵训练在捕捉序列级行为方面的不足，该研究提出一种面向完成分布统计特征匹配的目标函数，无需任务特定验证器即可提供密集语义反馈。为高效优化，提出能量化微调（EBFT）方法，通过分块并行采样并发生成多个 rollout，批量提取特征，并基于嵌入进行在线策略梯度更新。实验表明，EBFT在问答、无结构编码和翻译任务上表现与 RLVR 相当，超越监督微调的下游准确率，同时验证交叉熵低于两者方法。
Trust Your Critic: Robust Reward Modeling and Reinforcement Learning for Faithful Image Editing and Generation
Authors: Xiangyu Zhao, Peiyuan Zhang, Junming Lin, Tianhao Liang, Yuchen Duan, Shengyuan Ding, Changyao Tian, Yuhang Zang, Junchi Yan, Xue Yang
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing image editing and text-to-image (T2I) generation. However, current reward models, which act as critics during RL, often suffer from hallucinations and assign noisy scores, inherently misguiding the optimization process. In this paper, we present FIRM (Faithful Image Reward Modeling), a comprehensive framework that develops robust reward models to provide accurate and reliable guidance for faithful image generation and editing. First, we design tailored data curation pipelines to construct high-quality scoring datasets. Specifically, we evaluate editing using both execution and consistency, while generation is primarily assessed via instruction following. Using these pipelines, we collect the FIRM-Edit-370K and FIRM-Gen-293K datasets, and train specialized reward models (FIRM-Edit-8B and FIRM-Gen-8B) that accurately reflect these criteria. Second, we introduce FIRM-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark specifically designed for editing and generation critics. Evaluations demonstrate that our models achieve superior alignment with human judgment compared to existing metrics. Furthermore, to seamlessly integrate these critics into the RL pipeline, we formulate a novel "Base-and-Bonus" reward strategy that balances competing objectives: Consistency-Modulated Execution (CME) for editing and Quality-Modulated Alignment (QMA) for generation. Empowered by this framework, our resulting models FIRM-Qwen-Edit and FIRM-SD3.5 achieve substantial performance breakthroughs. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FIRM mitigates hallucinations, establishing a new standard for fidelity and instruction adherence over existing general models. All of our datasets, models, and code have been publicly available at https://firm-reward.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信任你的批评者：用于忠实图像编辑与生成的鲁棒奖励建模与强化学习
强化学习（RL）已成为提升图像编辑和文本到图像（T2I）生成的有前景范式。然而，当前作为RL中批评者的奖励模型常存在幻觉问题并赋予噪声评分，从而固有地误导优化过程。本文提出FIRM（忠实图像奖励建模），一个全面框架，旨在构建鲁棒奖励模型，为忠实图像生成与编辑提供准确可靠的指导。首先，我们设计定制化数据整理流程，构建高质量评分数据集。具体而言，编辑评估结合执行与一致性，生成则主要通过指令遵循评估。利用这些流程，我们收集了FIRM-Edit-370K和FIRM-Gen-293K数据集，并训练了专门的奖励模型（FIRM-Edit-8B和FIRM-Gen-8B），准确反映上述标准。其次，我们引入FIRM-Bench，专为编辑与生成批评者设计的综合基准。评估表明，我们的模型在与人类判断的一致性上优于现有指标。此外，为无缝集成这些批评者至RL流程，我们提出新颖的“基础与奖励”奖励策略，平衡竞争目标：编辑采用一致性调制执行（CME），生成采用质量调制对齐（QMA）。依托此框架，我们的模型FIRM-Qwen-Edit和FIRM-SD3.5实现显著性能突破。全面实验表明，FIRM有效缓解幻觉，为现有通用模型树立了新的忠实度与指令遵循标准。所有数据集、模型及代码均已公开于https://firm-reward.github.io。

It constructs high-quality datasets—FIRM-Edit-370K and FIRM-Gen-293K—through tailored curation pipelines evaluating execution, consistency, and instruction following, then trains specialized 8B-parameter reward models. FIRM-Bench, a new benchmark, validates superior human alignment. The "Base-and-Bonus" reward strategy balances objectives via CME for editing and QMA for generation, enabling FIRM-Qwen-Edit and FIRM-SD3.5 to achieve significant performance gains, reducing hallucinations and setting a new standard for fidelity and instruction adherence.
为解决当前奖励模型在图像编辑与生成中因幻觉和评分噪声导致的优化误导问题，本文提出FIRM框架，构建高质量数据集FIRM-Edit-370K与FIRM-Gen-293K，通过定制化数据筛选流程评估执行、一致性与指令遵循，训练专用8B参数奖励模型。FIRM-Bench基准验证其优于现有指标的人类对齐能力。创新的“基础+奖励”策略通过CME与QMA平衡编辑与生成目标，使FIRM-Qwen-Edit与FIRM-SD3.5实现显著性能提升，有效降低幻觉，确立了更高保真度与指令遵循的新标准。
Examining Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges in Non-Verifiable LLM Post-Training
Authors: Yixin Liu, Yue Yu, DiJia Su, Sid Wang, Xuewei Wang, Song Jiang, Bo Liu, Arman Cohan, Yuandong Tian, Zhengxing Chen
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges, which can benefit from inference-time scaling, provide a promising path for extending the success of reasoning models to non-verifiable domains where the output correctness/quality cannot be directly checked. However, while reasoning judges have shown better performance on static evaluation benchmarks, their effectiveness in actual policy training has not been systematically examined. Therefore, we conduct a rigorous study to investigate the actual impact of non-reasoning and reasoning judges in reinforcement-learning-based LLM alignment. Our controlled synthetic setting, where a "gold-standard" judge (gpt-oss-120b) provides preference annotations to train smaller judges, reveals key differences between non-reasoning and reasoning judges: non-reasoning judges lead to reward hacking easily, while reasoning judges can lead to policies that achieve strong performance when evaluated by the gold-standard judge. Interestingly, we find that the reasoning-judge-trained policies achieve such strong performance by learning to generate highly effective adversarial outputs that can also score well on popular benchmarks such as Arena-Hard by deceiving other LLM-judges. Combined with our further analysis, our study highlights both important findings and room for improvements for applying (reasoning) LLM-judges in non-verifiable LLM post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在不可验证的LLM后训练中考察推理LLM作为裁判的效果
推理LLM作为裁判，可受益于推理时的扩展，为将推理模型的成功扩展到输出正确性/质量无法直接验证的领域提供了有前景的路径。然而，尽管推理裁判在静态评估基准上表现更优，其在实际策略训练中的有效性尚未系统研究。因此，我们开展严谨研究，探讨非推理与推理裁判在基于强化学习的LLM对齐中的实际影响。在我们可控的合成设置中，由“黄金标准”裁判（gpt-oss-120b）提供偏好标注以训练较小裁判，揭示了非推理与推理裁判的关键差异：非推理裁判易导致奖励作弊，而推理裁判可生成在黄金标准裁判评估下表现优异的策略。有趣的是，我们发现推理裁判训练的策略通过学习生成高度有效的对抗性输出，在欺骗其他LLM裁判的同时，也能在Arena-Hard等流行基准上获得高分。结合进一步分析，本研究既突出了应用（推理）LLM裁判于不可验证LLM后训练的重要发现，也指出了改进空间。
Summary / 总结

Using a controlled synthetic environment where a gold-standard judge (gpt-oss-120b) trains smaller judges, the researchers find that non-reasoning judges frequently lead to reward hacking, while reasoning judges produce policies that achieve strong performance when evaluated by the gold-standard judge. Notably, these reasoning-judge-trained policies succeed by generating adversarial outputs that deceive other LLM-judges and score well on benchmarks like Arena-Hard, suggesting both potential and risks in deploying reasoning judges for alignment in non-verifiable domains.
为探究推理型LLM作为裁判在无法直接验证输出正确性的后训练场景中的有效性，本研究系统比较了推理型与非推理型裁判在基于强化学习的对齐任务中的表现。在由黄金标准裁判（gpt-oss-120b）训练小型裁判的受控合成环境中，研究发现非推理裁判易导致奖励欺骗，而推理裁判则能生成在黄金标准裁判评估下表现优异的策略。有趣的是，这些推理裁判训练的策略通过生成能欺骗其他LLM裁判并获得高分（如Arena-Hard）的对抗性输出实现成功，揭示了在非可验证领域应用推理裁判的潜力与风险。
One Model, Many Budgets: Elastic Latent Interfaces for Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Moayed Haji-Ali, Willi Menapace, Ivan Skorokhodov, Dogyun Park, Anil Kag, Michael Vasilkovsky, Sergey Tulyakov, Vicente Ordonez, Aliaksandr Siarohin
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/elit/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve high generative quality but lock FLOPs to image resolution, limiting principled latency-quality trade-offs, and allocate computation uniformly across input spatial tokens, wasting resource allocation to unimportant regions. We introduce Elastic Latent Interface Transformer (ELIT), a drop-in, DiT-compatible mechanism that decouples input image size from compute. Our approach inserts a latent interface, a learnable variable-length token sequence on which standard transformer blocks can operate. Lightweight Read and Write cross-attention layers move information between spatial tokens and latents and prioritize important input regions. By training with random dropping of tail latents, ELIT learns to produce importance-ordered representations with earlier latents capturing global structure while later ones contain information to refine details. At inference, the number of latents can be dynamically adjusted to match compute constraints. ELIT is deliberately minimal, adding two cross-attention layers while leaving the rectified flow objective and the DiT stack unchanged. Across datasets and architectures (DiT, U-ViT, HDiT, MM-DiT), ELIT delivers consistent gains. On ImageNet-1K 512px, ELIT delivers an average gain of $35.3\%$ and $39.6\%$ in FID and FDD scores. Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/elit/
中文标题/摘要
标题：一个模型，多种预算：用于扩散变换器的弹性潜在接口
扩散变换器（DiTs）虽能实现高质量生成，但其FLOPs固定于图像分辨率，限制了延迟与质量的原理性权衡，并在输入空间令牌上均匀分配计算，浪费在不重要区域的资源。我们引入弹性潜在接口变换器（ELIT），一种可直接替换、兼容DiT的机制，将输入图像尺寸与计算解耦。该方法插入一个潜在接口，即一个可学习的可变长度令牌序列，标准变换器块可在此序列上操作。轻量级读写交叉注意力层在空间令牌与潜在表示间传递信息，并优先处理重要输入区域。通过随机丢弃尾部潜在表示进行训练，ELIT学会生成按重要性排序的表示，早期潜在表示捕捉全局结构，后期则包含细化细节的信息。推理时，潜在表示数量可动态调整以匹配计算约束。ELIT设计极简，仅增加两层交叉注意力，而保留修正流目标和DiT堆栈不变。在多个数据集和架构（DiT、U-ViT、HDiT、MM-DiT）上，ELIT均带来一致增益。在ImageNet-1K 512px上，ELIT平均提升FID和FDD评分35.3%和39.6%。项目页面：https://snap-research.github.io/elit/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigid computation-to-resolution coupling in diffusion transformers that hinders efficient latency-quality trade-offs, the authors propose ELIT, a minimal drop-in mechanism that decouples image size from compute by introducing a learnable latent interface. This interface, a variable-length token sequence, enables transformer blocks to operate on latents while lightweight read/write cross-attention layers transfer and prioritize information from spatial tokens. Training with random latent dropout encourages the model to encode global structure early and fine details later, allowing dynamic inference-time adjustment of latents to match compute budgets. Across multiple DiT variants and datasets, ELIT consistently improves quality: on ImageNet-1K 512px, it reduces FID by 35.3% and FDD by 39.6% without altering the core diffusion objective or architecture.
为了解决扩散Transformer中计算量与图像分辨率强绑定、难以实现延迟-质量权衡的问题，作者提出ELIT机制，通过引入可学习的潜变量接口，将图像尺寸与计算成本解耦。该接口通过轻量级读写交叉注意力层实现空间令牌与潜变量间的信息传递，并支持推理时动态调整潜变量数量以适配计算预算。通过随机丢弃尾部潜变量训练，ELIT学会按重要性顺序编码：早期潜变量捕捉全局结构，后期潜变量负责细节优化。在多个DiT架构和数据集上，ELIT表现稳定：在ImageNet-1K 512px上，FID降低35.3%，FDD降低39.6%，且未修改核心Transformer结构或训练目标。
Separable neural architectures as a primitive for unified predictive and generative intelligence
Authors: Reza T. Batley, Apurba Sarker, Rajib Mostakim, Andrew Klichine, Sourav Saha
First: 2026-03-12T17:56:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:56:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intelligent systems across physics, language and perception often exhibit factorisable structure, yet are typically modelled by monolithic neural architectures that do not explicitly exploit this structure. The separable neural architecture (SNA) addresses this by formalising a representational class that unifies additive, quadratic and tensor-decomposed neural models. By constraining interaction order and tensor rank, SNAs impose a structural inductive bias that factorises high-dimensional mappings into low-arity components. Separability need not be a property of the system itself: it often emerges in the coordinates or representations through which the system is expressed. Crucially, this coordinate-aware formulation reveals a structural analogy between chaotic spatiotemporal dynamics and linguistic autoregression. By treating continuous physical states as smooth, separable embeddings, SNAs enable distributional modelling of chaotic systems. This approach mitigates the nonphysical drift characteristics of deterministic operators whilst remaining applicable to discrete sequences. The compositional versatility of this approach is demonstrated across four domains: autonomous waypoint navigation via reinforcement learning, inverse generation of multifunctional microstructures, distributional modelling of turbulent flow and neural language modelling. These results establish the separable neural architecture as a domain-agnostic primitive for predictive and generative intelligence, capable of unifying both deterministic and distributional representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可分离神经架构作为统一预测与生成智能的基本构件
在物理、语言和感知等领域，智能系统常表现出可分解的结构，但通常被建模为单一的神经架构，未显式利用该结构。可分离神经架构（SNA）通过形式化一个统一加性、二次型和张量分解神经模型的表示类来解决此问题。通过约束交互阶数和张量秩，SNA施加了一种结构性归纳偏置，将高维映射分解为低元组分量。可分离性不必是系统本身的属性：它常在系统所表达的坐标或表示中显现。关键的是，这种坐标感知的表述揭示了混沌时空动力学与语言自回归之间在结构上的类比。通过将连续物理状态视为平滑、可分离的嵌入，SNA实现了对混沌系统的分布建模。该方法缓解了确定性算子的非物理漂移特性，同时仍适用于离散序列。该方法的组合灵活性在四个领域得到验证：通过强化学习实现自主航点导航、多功能微结构的逆向生成、湍流流动的分布建模及神经语言建模。这些结果确立了可分离神经架构作为预测与生成智能的领域无关基本构件，能够统一确定性和分布性表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that intelligent systems across domains often possess factorisable structure yet are typically modeled by monolithic neural networks, the paper introduces separable neural architectures (SNAs) as a formal class that unifies additive, quadratic, and tensor-decomposed models. By imposing constraints on interaction order and tensor rank, SNAs induce an inductive bias that decomposes high-dimensional mappings into low-arity components, enabling efficient representation. HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies
Authors: Amber Xie, Haozhi Qi, Dorsa Sadigh
First: 2026-03-12T17:56:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:56:29+00:00
Comments: Website: https://amberxie88.github.io/handelbot
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HandelBot：通过灵巧机器人策略的快速适应实现真实世界钢琴演奏
几十年来，掌握多指灵巧操作一直是机器人领域的重大挑战。尽管其潜力巨大，但高质量数据的收集难度仍是高精度任务的主要瓶颈。虽然强化学习和仿真到现实的迁移提供了有前景的替代方案，但迁移后的策略往往无法胜任要求毫米级精度的任务，例如双手弹钢琴。在本研究中，我们提出了HandelBot框架，该框架结合了仿真策略与通过两阶段流程实现的快速适应。从仿真训练的策略出发，我们首先应用结构化优化阶段，通过调整横向手指关节以纠正空间对齐，基于物理滚动进行调整；随后，我们使用残差强化学习自主学习精细的修正动作。通过在五首公认歌曲上进行大量硬件实验，我们证明HandelBot能够成功实现精确的双手钢琴演奏。我们的系统性能比直接部署仿真策略高出1.8倍，且仅需30分钟的物理交互数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving millimeter-scale precision in dexterous robotic manipulation, particularly for bimanual piano playing, this work introduces HandelBot: a two-stage framework that adapts simulation-trained policies to real-world hardware. The method first refines spatial alignment via structured adjustments to lateral finger joints using physical rollouts, then employs residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. BiGain: Unified Token Compression for Joint Generation and Classification
Authors: Jiacheng Liu, Shengkun Tang, Jiacheng Cui, Dongkuan Xu, Zhiqiang Shen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:53+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Code: https://github.com/Greenoso/BiGain
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Acceleration methods for diffusion models (e.g., token merging or downsampling) typically optimize synthesis quality under reduced compute, yet often ignore discriminative capacity. We revisit token compression with a joint objective and present BiGain, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that preserves generation quality while improving classification in accelerated diffusion models. Our key insight is frequency separation: mapping feature-space signals into a frequency-aware representation disentangles fine detail from global semantics, enabling compression that respects both generative fidelity and discriminative utility. BiGain reflects this principle with two frequency-aware operators: (1) Laplacian-gated token merging, which encourages merges among spectrally smooth tokens while discouraging merges of high-contrast tokens, thereby retaining edges and textures; and (2) Interpolate-Extrapolate KV Downsampling, which downsamples keys/values via a controllable interextrapolation between nearest and average pooling while keeping queries intact, thereby conserving attention precision. Across DiT- and U-Net-based backbones and ImageNet-1K, ImageNet-100, Oxford-IIIT Pets, and COCO-2017, our operators consistently improve the speed-accuracy trade-off for diffusion-based classification, while maintaining or enhancing generation quality under comparable acceleration. For instance, on ImageNet-1K, with 70% token merging on Stable Diffusion 2.0, BiGain increases classification accuracy by 7.15% while improving FID by 0.34 (1.85%). Our analyses indicate that balanced spectral retention, preserving high-frequency detail and low/mid-frequency semantics, is a reliable design rule for token compression in diffusion models. To our knowledge, BiGain is the first framework to jointly study and advance both generation and classification under accelerated diffusion, supporting lower-cost deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BiGain：面向联合生成与分类的统一令牌压缩
扩散模型的加速方法（如令牌合并或下采样）通常在减少计算的同时优化合成质量，但往往忽略判别能力。我们重新审视令牌压缩，提出BiGain，一个无需训练、即插即用的框架，可在加速扩散模型中保持生成质量的同时提升分类性能。我们的核心洞察是频域分离：将特征空间信号映射为频域感知表示，将细粒度细节与全局语义解耦，从而实现兼顾生成保真度与判别效用的压缩。BiGain通过两个频域感知操作体现该原则：（1）拉普拉斯门控令牌合并，鼓励频谱平滑令牌合并，抑制高对比度令牌合并，从而保留边缘和纹理；（2）插值-外推KV下采样，通过可控的最近邻与平均池化之间的插值外推下采样键值，同时保持查询不变，从而保留注意力精度。在DiT和U-Net架构及ImageNet-1K、ImageNet-100、Oxford-IIIT Pets和COCO-2017数据集上，我们的操作始终改善扩散模型分类的速效权衡，同时在同等加速条件下保持或提升生成质量。例如，在ImageNet-1K上，对Stable Diffusion 2.0进行70%令牌合并时，BiGain分类准确率提升7.15%，FID提升0.34（1.85%）。我们的分析表明，平衡频谱保留——保留高频细节与低/中频语义——是扩散模型令牌压缩的可靠设计准则。据我们所知，BiGain是首个同时研究并推进加速扩散模型下生成与分类的框架，支持低成本部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between synthesis quality and discriminative capacity in accelerated diffusion models, BiGain introduces a training-free, plug-and-play framework that jointly optimizes generation and classification through frequency-aware token compression. It employs two operators: Laplacian-gated token merging, which preserves high-contrast features by discouraging merges of spectrally distinct tokens, and Interpolate-Extrapolate KV Downsampling, which selectively downsamples keys/values while retaining query precision. Across multiple backbones and datasets, BiGain consistently improves the speed-accuracy trade-off without degrading generation quality; for example, on ImageNet-1K with 70% token merging, it boosts classification accuracy by 7.15% while improving FID by 0.34 (1.85%). The results validate that balanced spectral retention—preserving high-frequency detail and low/mid-frequency semantics—is critical for effective token compression in diffusion models.
为弥合加速扩散模型中生成质量与判别能力之间的差距，BiGain 提出一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过频域感知的令牌压缩联合优化生成与分类。其包含两个操作：拉普拉斯门控令牌合并，通过抑制频谱差异大的令牌合并保留高对比度特征；以及插值-外推 KV 下采样，选择性下采样键值对同时保留查询精度。在多个骨干网络和数据集上，BiGain 一致提升速度-精度权衡，且不降低生成质量；例如在 ImageNet-1K 上 70% 令牌合并时，分类准确率提升 7.15%，FID 提升 0.34（1.85%）。实验表明，平衡频谱保留——同时保留高频细节与低/中频语义——是扩散模型令牌压缩的关键设计原则。
STAMP: Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy
Authors: Fengwei Tian, Payel Bhattacharjee, Heidi Hanson, Geoffrey D. Rubin, Joseph Y. Lo, Ravi Tandon
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00
Comments: EACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present STAMP (Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy), a new framework for task-aware text privatization that achieves an improved privacy-utility trade-off. STAMP selectively allocates privacy budgets across tokens by jointly considering (i) each token's importance to the downstream task (as measured via a task- or query-specific representation), and (ii) its privacy sensitivity (e.g., names, dates, identifiers). This token-level partitioning enables fine-grained, group-wise control over the level of noise applied to different parts of the input, balancing privacy protection with task relevance. To privatize individual token embeddings, we introduce the polar mechanism, which perturbs only the direction of embeddings on the unit sphere while preserving their magnitude. Decoding is performed via cosine nearest-neighbor search, aligning the perturbation geometry with the decoding geometry. Unlike isotropic noise mechanisms, the polar mechanism maintains semantic neighborhoods in the embedding space and better preserves downstream utility. Experimental evaluations on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News datasets demonstrate that STAMP, when combined with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently achieves superior privacy-utility trade-offs across varying per-token privacy budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STAMP：面向文本隐私的自适应任务感知机制
我们提出STAMP（面向文本隐私的自适应任务感知机制），一种新的任务感知文本隐私化框架，实现了更优的隐私-效用权衡。STAMP通过联合考虑（i）每个token对下游任务的重要性（通过任务或查询特定表示度量），以及（ii）其隐私敏感性（如姓名、日期、标识符），选择性地在token间分配隐私预算。这种token级划分实现了对输入不同部分施加噪声程度的细粒度、分组控制，平衡隐私保护与任务相关性。为对单个token嵌入进行隐私化，我们引入极坐标机制，仅扰动单位球面上嵌入的方向，同时保持其幅度不变。解码通过余弦最近邻搜索实现，使扰动几何与解码几何对齐。与各向同性噪声机制不同，极坐标机制在嵌入空间中保持语义邻域，更好地保留下游效用。在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，结合归一化极坐标机制的STAMP，在不同per-token隐私预算下均能持续实现更优的隐私-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance privacy and utility in text processing, STAMP introduces a task-aware framework that allocates privacy budgets selectively per token based on both task relevance and privacy sensitivity. It employs a polar mechanism to perturb token embeddings by altering their direction on the unit sphere while preserving magnitude, enabling semantic neighborhood preservation during decoding via cosine nearest-neighbor search. Experiments on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News show that STAMP with the normalized polar mechanism consistently outperforms baseline methods in privacy-utility trade-offs across varying token-level privacy budgets.
为在文本处理中平衡隐私与效用，STAMP提出一种任务感知框架，按每个词元的下游任务相关性与隐私敏感性动态分配隐私预算。其采用极坐标机制，仅扰动词元嵌入的方向（保持模长不变），并通过余弦最近邻搜索解码，保留语义邻域结构。在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，STAMP结合归一化极坐标机制，在不同词元隐私预算下均能显著优于基线方法，实现更优的隐私-效用权衡。
SceneAssistant: A Visual Feedback Agent for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Generation
Authors: Jun Luo, Jiaxiang Tang, Ruijie Lu, Gang Zeng
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-3D scene generation from natural language is highly desirable for digital content creation. However, existing methods are largely domain-restricted or reliant on predefined spatial relationships, limiting their capacity for unconstrained, open-vocabulary 3D scene synthesis. In this paper, we introduce SceneAssistant, a visual-feedback-driven agent designed for open-vocabulary 3D scene generation. Our framework leverages modern 3D object generation model along with the spatial reasoning and planning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). To enable open-vocabulary scene composition, we provide the VLMs with a comprehensive set of atomic operations (e.g., Scale, Rotate, FocusOn). At each interaction step, the VLM receives rendered visual feedback and takes actions accordingly, iteratively refining the scene to achieve more coherent spatial arrangements and better alignment with the input text. Experimental results demonstrate that our method can generate diverse, open-vocabulary, and high-quality 3D scenes. Both qualitative analysis and quantitative human evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach over existing methods. Furthermore, our method allows users to instruct the agent to edit existing scenes based on natural language commands. Our code is available at https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
中文标题/摘要
标题：SceneAssistant：面向开放词汇3D场景生成的视觉反馈代理
从自然语言生成3D场景对数字内容创作具有高度需求。然而，现有方法大多受限于特定领域或依赖预定义的空间关系，限制了其在无约束、开放词汇3D场景合成中的能力。本文提出SceneAssistant，一种面向开放词汇3D场景生成的视觉反馈驱动代理。我们的框架结合现代3D物体生成模型与视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的空间推理和规划能力。为实现开放词汇场景组合，我们为VLMs提供一套完整的原子操作（如缩放、旋转、聚焦）。在每次交互步骤中，VLM接收渲染后的视觉反馈并据此采取行动，迭代优化场景，以实现更连贯的空间布局和更贴近输入文本的表达。实验结果表明，我们的方法可生成多样、开放词汇且高质量的3D场景。定性分析与定量人工评估均证明我们的方法优于现有方法。此外，我们的方法允许用户通过自然语言指令指导代理编辑现有场景。代码已开源：https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for flexible, open-vocabulary 3D scene generation from natural language, SceneAssistant introduces a visual-feedback agent that combines 3D object generation models with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) capable of spatial reasoning. The agent is equipped with atomic operations like Scale, Rotate, and FocusOn, enabling iterative refinement of 3D scenes based on rendered visual feedback at each step. Experiments show that this approach produces diverse, high-quality scenes that better align with input text compared to existing methods, and it also supports natural-language editing of existing scenes, as confirmed by both qualitative and quantitative human evaluations.
为实现无需预设空间关系、支持开放词汇的自然语言驱动3D场景生成，作者提出SceneAssistant，该视觉反馈驱动代理结合3D物体生成模型与视觉语言模型（VLM），赋予VLM缩放、旋转、聚焦等原子操作能力，通过迭代接收视觉反馈逐步优化空间布局与文本对齐。实验表明，该方法生成的3D场景多样且高质量，在定性与定量评估中优于现有方法，同时支持用户通过自然语言指令编辑现有场景。
Incremental Neural Network Verification via Learned Conflicts
Authors: Raya Elsaleh, Liam Davis, Haoze Wu, Guy Katz
First: 2026-03-12T17:52:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:52:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural network verification is often used as a core component within larger analysis procedures, which generate sequences of closely related verification queries over the same network. In existing neural network verifiers, each query is typically solved independently, and information learned during previous runs is discarded, leading to repeated exploration of the same infeasible regions of the search space. In this work, we aim to expedite verification by reducing this redundancy. We propose an incremental verification technique that reuses learned conflicts across related verification queries. The technique can be added on top of any branch-and-bound-based neural network verifier. During verification, the verifier records conflicts corresponding to learned infeasible combinations of activation phases, and retains them across runs. We formalize a refinement relation between verification queries and show that conflicts learned for a query remain valid under refinement, enabling sound conflict inheritance. Inherited conflicts are handled using a SAT solver to perform consistency checks and propagation, allowing infeasible subproblems to be detected and pruned early during search. We implement the proposed technique in the Marabou verifier and evaluate it on three verification tasks: local robustness radius determination, verification with input splitting, and minimal sufficient feature set extraction. Our experiments show that incremental conflict reuse reduces verification effort and yields speedups of up to $1.9\times$ over a non-incremental baseline.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of solving neural network verification queries independently, this work proposes an incremental verification method that reuses conflicts learned across related queries to avoid redundant search. The technique integrates with any branch-and-bound verifier by recording and preserving infeasible activation phase combinations as conflicts, which remain valid under query refinement. These conflicts are then leveraged via a SAT solver to prune infeasible subproblems early, reducing overall verification effort. Evaluated on three tasks including robustness radius computation and feature set extraction, the method achieves up to 1.9x speedup over a non-incremental baseline.
为解决神经网络验证中重复探索相同不可行区域的问题，本文提出一种增量验证方法，可在不同但相关的验证查询间复用已学习的冲突。该方法兼容任何基于分支定界框架的验证器，通过记录并保留激活阶段组合的不可行冲突，这些冲突在查询细化后仍保持有效性。利用SAT求解器对继承的冲突进行一致性检查与传播，提前剪枝不可行子问题，从而减少搜索开销。在三项任务中，该方法相较非增量基线最高提速1.9倍。
Temporal Straightening for Latent Planning
Authors: Ying Wang, Oumayma Bounou, Gaoyue Zhou, Randall Balestriero, Tim G. J. Rudner, Yann LeCun, Mengye Ren
First: 2026-03-12T17:49:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:49:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning good representations is essential for latent planning with world models. While pretrained visual encoders produce strong semantic visual features, they are not tailored to planning and contain information irrelevant -- or even detrimental -- to planning. Inspired by the perceptual straightening hypothesis in human visual processing, we introduce temporal straightening to improve representation learning for latent planning. Using a curvature regularizer that encourages locally straightened latent trajectories, we jointly learn an encoder and a predictor. We show that reducing curvature this way makes the Euclidean distance in latent space a better proxy for the geodesic distance and improves the conditioning of the planning objective. We demonstrate empirically that temporal straightening makes gradient-based planning more stable and yields significantly higher success rates across a suite of goal-reaching tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于潜在规划的时间直化
学习良好的表征对于世界模型的潜在规划至关重要。虽然预训练的视觉编码器能生成强大的语义视觉特征，但它们并未针对规划进行优化，且包含与规划无关甚至有害的信息。受人类视觉处理中感知直化假说的启发，我们引入时间直化以提升潜在规划的表征学习。通过一个曲率正则化器，鼓励局部直化的潜在轨迹，我们联合学习编码器和预测器。我们表明，通过这种方式降低曲率，能使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地线距离，并改善规划目标的条件数。实验证明，时间直化使基于梯度的规划更加稳定，并在一系列目标到达任务中显著提升了成功率。

To enhance latent planning, the paper addresses the limitation that pretrained visual encoders, though semantically strong, are not optimized for planning and may contain irrelevant or harmful information.
