鉴于大语言模型在工程上取得显著成功但理论基础仍显薄弱，本文提出基于生命周期的分类框架，将研究划分为数据准备、模型构建、训练、对齐、推理与评估六个阶段。系统梳理了驱动模型性能的核心理论机制，包括数据混合的数学依据、架构表征能力的理论边界及对齐算法的优化动态。同时指出前沿挑战，如合成数据自我改进的理论极限、安全保证的数学边界及涌现智能的机制根源，旨在为LLM研究从经验性工程向科学化发展提供系统路径。
LifeSim: Long-Horizon User Life Simulator for Personalized Assistant Evaluation
Authors: Feiyu Duan, Xuanjing Huang, Zhongyu Wei
First: 2026-03-12T16:49:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:49:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has accelerated progress toward universal AI assistants. However, existing benchmarks for personalized assistants remain misaligned with real-world user-assistant interactions, failing to capture the complexity of external contexts and users' cognitive states. To bridge this gap, we propose LifeSim, a user simulator that models user cognition through the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) model within physical environments for coherent life trajectories generation, and simulates intention-driven user interactive behaviors. Based on LifeSim, we introduce LifeSim-Eval, a comprehensive benchmark for multi-scenario, long-horizon personalized assistance. LifeSim-Eval covers 8 life domains and 1,200 diverse scenarios, and adopts a multi-turn interactive method to assess models' abilities to complete explicit and implicit intentions, recover user profiles, and produce high-quality responses. Under both single-scenario and long-horizon settings, our experiments reveal that current LLMs face significant limitations in handling implicit intention and long-term user preference modeling.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between current personalized assistant benchmarks and real-world user interactions, this work introduces LifeSim, a user simulator that models cognition via the Belief-Desire-Intention framework within physical environments to generate coherent life trajectories and simulate intention-driven behaviors. Built upon LifeSim, LifeSim-Eval is a comprehensive benchmark covering 8 life domains and 1,200 diverse scenarios, evaluating models through multi-turn interactions to assess their ability to fulfill explicit and implicit intentions, recover user profiles, and generate high-quality responses. Experimental results show that existing LLMs struggle significantly with modeling implicit intentions and long-term user preferences across long-horizon interactions.
为弥合现有个性化助手评测基准与真实用户交互之间的差距，本文提出LifeSim用户模拟器，通过信念-愿望-意图（BDI）模型在物理环境中模拟用户认知，生成连贯生活轨迹并模拟意图驱动行为。基于此，构建了覆盖8个生活领域、1200个多样化场景的综合性评测基准LifeSim-Eval，通过多轮交互评估模型完成显性/隐性意图、用户画像恢复及高质量回复的能力。实验表明，当前大语言模型在隐性意图理解与长期偏好建模方面存在显著局限，无论单场景还是长时程设置下表现均不佳。
IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL
Authors: Zhoujun Cheng, Yutao Xie, Yuxiao Qu, Amrith Setlur, Shibo Hao, Varad Pimpalkhute, Tongtong Liang, Feng Yao, Zhengzhong Liu, Eric Xing, Virginia Smith, Ruslan Salakhutdinov, Zhiting Hu, Taylor Killian, Aviral Kumar
First: 2026-03-12T16:49:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:49:21+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 27 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of clear scaling guidelines for reinforcement learning post-training of large language models, this work formulates compute-optimized sampling allocation as an optimization over three key resources: parallel rollouts per problem, problems per batch, and update steps. Experiments across diverse models and data distributions reveal that the optimal number of parallel rollouts grows with compute budget but eventually saturates, with different underlying mechanisms—solution sharpening for easier tasks and coverage expansion for harder ones. Increasing parallel rollouts reduces problem interference, while batch size primarily influences training stability and can vary widely without significant impact. The findings offer actionable, compute-efficient allocation rules for LLM RL post-training.
为解决大语言模型强化学习后训练阶段缺乏明确算力分配指南的问题，该研究将采样算力优化建模为对三个关键资源（每个问题的并行 rollout 数、批次内问题数、更新步数）的计算约束优化。实验表明，最优并行 rollout 数随算力预算增加而上升，最终趋于饱和，其机制在简单与困难任务中不同——前者靠解精炼，后者靠覆盖扩展。增加并行 rollout 可降低任务间干扰，而批次内问题数主要影响训练稳定性，可在较宽范围内调整。结果为 LLM 强化学习后训练提供了可操作的高效算力分配准则。
Linking Perception, Confidence and Accuracy in MLLMs
Authors: Yuetian Du, Yucheng Wang, Rongyu Zhang, Zhijie Xu, Boyu Yang, Ming Kong, Jie Liu, Qiang Zhu
First: 2026-03-12T16:47:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:47:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have predominantly focused on enhancing visual perception to improve accuracy. However, a critical question remains unexplored: Do models know when they do not know? Through a probing experiment, we reveal a severe confidence miscalibration problem in MLLMs. To address this, we propose Confidence-Driven Reinforcement Learning (CDRL), which uses original-noise image pairs and a novel confidence-based reward to enhance perceptual sensitivity and robustly calibrate the model's confidence. Beyond training benefits, calibrated confidence enables more effective test-time scaling as a free lunch. We further propose Confidence-Aware Test-Time Scaling (CA-TTS), which dynamically coordinates Self-Consistency, Self-Reflection, and Visual Self-Check modules guided by confidence signals. An Expert Model acts in multiple roles (e.g., Planner, Critic, Voter) to schedule these modules and provide external verification. Our integrated framework establishes new state-of-the-art results with consistent 8.8% gains across four benchmarks. More ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of each module and scaling superiority.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态大语言模型中感知、置信度与准确性的关联
近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的研究主要聚焦于提升视觉感知以提高准确性。然而，一个关键问题尚未被探索：模型是否知道自身何时“不知道”？通过探针实验，我们揭示了MLLMs中严重的置信度误校准问题。为此，我们提出置信度驱动的强化学习（CDRL），利用原始噪声图像对和新颖的置信度奖励机制，增强感知敏感性并稳健校准模型置信度。除训练收益外，校准后的置信度还能作为“免费午餐”实现更有效的测试时扩展。我们进一步提出置信度感知的测试时扩展（CA-TTS），通过置信度信号动态协调自一致性、自反思与视觉自检模块。专家模型扮演多种角色（如规划者、批评者、投票者），调度这些模块并提供外部验证。我们的集成框架在四个基准测试中均取得一致8.8%的性能提升，创下新SOTA。更多消融实验验证了各模块的有效性及扩展优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of self-awareness in MLLMs regarding their knowledge limits, this work investigates confidence miscalibration through probing experiments. It introduces Confidence-Driven Reinforcement Learning (CDRL) to improve perceptual sensitivity and calibrate confidence using original-noise image pairs and confidence-based rewards. Additionally, it proposes Confidence-Aware Test-Time Scaling (CA-TTS) to dynamically coordinate self-consistency, self-reflection, and visual self-check modules guided by confidence signals, with an Expert Model orchestrating these components. Experiments show the integrated framework achieves consistent 8.8% gains across four benchmarks, demonstrating both training and test-time benefits.
鉴于当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）缺乏对自身知识边界的认识，该研究通过探针实验揭示了模型置信度校准严重不足的问题，并提出基于置信度驱动的强化学习（CDRL）方法，利用原始噪声图像对与置信度奖励机制提升感知敏感度并校准置信度。进一步提出的置信度感知测试时扩展（CA-TTS）方法，依据置信度动态协调自一致性、自反思与视觉自检模块，由专家模型扮演规划者、批评者与投票者角色提供外部验证。实验表明，该集成框架在四个基准测试中均实现稳定8.8%的性能提升，验证了训练与测试阶段各模块的有效性与扩展优势。
EgoIntent: An Egocentric Step-level Benchmark for Understanding What, Why, and Next
Authors: Ye Pan, Chi Kit Wong, Yuanhuiyi Lyu, Hanqian Li, Jiahao Huo, Jiacheng Chen, Lutao Jiang, Xu Zheng, Xuming Hu
First: 2026-03-12T16:46:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:46:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable video reasoning capabilities across diverse tasks. However, their ability to understand human intent at a fine-grained level in egocentric videos remains largely unexplored. Existing benchmarks focus primarily on episode-level intent reasoning, overlooking the finer granularity of step-level intent understanding. Yet applications such as intelligent assistants, robotic imitation learning, and augmented reality guidance require understanding not only what a person is doing at each step, but also why and what comes next, in order to provide timely and context-aware support. To this end, we introduce EgoIntent, a step-level intent understanding benchmark for egocentric videos. It comprises 3,014 steps spanning 15 diverse indoor and outdoor daily-life scenarios, and evaluates models on three complementary dimensions: local intent (What), global intent (Why), and next-step plan (Next). Crucially, each clip is truncated immediately before the key outcome of the queried step (e.g., contact or grasp) occurs and contains no frames from subsequent steps, preventing future-frame leakage and enabling a clean evaluation of anticipatory step understanding and next-step planning. We evaluate 15 MLLMs, including both state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source models. Even the best-performing model achieves an average score of only 33.31 across the three intent dimensions, underscoring that step-level intent understanding in egocentric videos remains a highly challenging problem that calls for further investigation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fine-grained intent understanding in egocentric videos for applications like robotics and AR, the authors introduce EgoIntent, a step-level benchmark evaluating what, why, and next actions. The dataset comprises 3,014 steps across 15 real-world scenarios, with clips truncated just before key action outcomes to prevent future-frame leakage. Evaluating 15 MLLMs, including top open-source and closed-source models, the study finds that even the best model scores only 33.31 on average across the three intent dimensions, highlighting the significant challenge of step-level intent reasoning in egocentric video understanding.
为满足智能助手、机器人模仿学习和AR引导等应用对细粒度意图理解的需求，作者提出了EgoIntent，一个面向第一人称视频的步级意图理解基准，评估三个维度：局部意图（What）、全局意图（Why）和下一步计划（Next）。该数据集包含15个日常场景中的3014个步骤，每个视频片段在关键动作发生前截断，避免未来帧泄露。评估15个MLLM模型后发现，表现最好的模型在三个维度上的平均得分仅为33.31，表明在第一人称视频中实现步级意图理解仍是一个极具挑战性的问题。
FlashMotion: Few-Step Controllable Video Generation with Trajectory Guidance
Authors: Quanhao Li, Zhen Xing, Rui Wang, Haidong Cao, Qi Dai, Daoguo Dong, Zuxuan Wu
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in trajectory-controllable video generation have achieved remarkable progress. Previous methods mainly use adapter-based architectures for precise motion control along predefined trajectories. However, all these methods rely on a multi-step denoising process, leading to substantial time redundancy and computational overhead. While existing video distillation methods successfully distill multi-step generators into few-step, directly applying these approaches to trajectory-controllable video generation results in noticeable degradation in both video quality and trajectory accuracy. To bridge this gap, we introduce FlashMotion, a novel training framework designed for few-step trajectory-controllable video generation. We first train a trajectory adapter on a multi-step video generator for precise trajectory control. Then, we distill the generator into a few-step version to accelerate video generation. Finally, we finetune the adapter using a hybrid strategy that combines diffusion and adversarial objectives, aligning it with the few-step generator to produce high-quality, trajectory-accurate videos. For evaluation, we introduce FlashBench, a benchmark for long-sequence trajectory-controllable video generation that measures both video quality and trajectory accuracy across varying numbers of foreground objects. Experiments on two adapter architectures show that FlashMotion surpasses existing video distillation methods and previous multi-step models in both visual quality and trajectory consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashMotion：基于轨迹引导的少步可控视频生成
近期轨迹可控视频生成领域取得显著进展。此前方法主要采用适配器架构，沿预定义轨迹实现精确运动控制，但均依赖多步去噪过程，导致时间冗余和计算开销大。现有视频蒸馏方法虽成功将多步生成器蒸馏为少步版本，但直接应用于轨迹可控视频生成时，视频质量和轨迹精度均明显下降。为弥合此差距，我们提出FlashMotion，一种专为少步轨迹可控视频生成设计的新型训练框架。首先，我们在多步视频生成器上训练轨迹适配器以实现精确轨迹控制；接着，将生成器蒸馏为少步版本以加速视频生成；最后，采用结合扩散与对抗目标的混合策略微调适配器，使其与少步生成器对齐，生成高质量且轨迹精确的视频。为评估，我们引入FlashBench，一个衡量长序列轨迹可控视频生成中视频质量与轨迹精度的基准，支持不同前景物体数量。在两种适配器架构上的实验表明，FlashMotion在视觉质量和轨迹一致性上均优于现有蒸馏方法和先前的多步模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of multi-step trajectory-controllable video generation, FlashMotion introduces a novel training framework that distills a multi-step video generator into a few-step version while preserving trajectory accuracy. The method first trains a trajectory adapter on the multi-step generator, then distills the generator into a faster few-step model, and finally fine-tunes the adapter using a hybrid diffusion and adversarial objective to align with the distilled generator. Experimental results on FlashBench, a new benchmark for long-sequence trajectory control, demonstrate that FlashMotion outperforms existing distillation methods and prior multi-step models in both video quality and trajectory consistency across varying foreground object counts.
为解决多步轨迹可控视频生成效率低的问题，FlashMotion提出了一种新颖的训练框架，将多步视频生成器蒸馏为少步版本同时保持轨迹精度。该方法首先在多步生成器上训练轨迹适配器，随后蒸馏生成器为少步版本，最后采用混合扩散对抗策略微调适配器，使其与蒸馏生成器对齐。实验表明，FlashMotion在FlashBench新基准上，无论前景物体数量如何变化，均在视频质量和轨迹一致性上优于现有蒸馏方法和先前的多步模型。
Automatic Generation of High-Performance RL Environments
Authors: Seth Karten, Rahul Dev Appapogu, Chi Jin
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:47+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 9 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Translating complex reinforcement learning (RL) environments into high-performance implementations has traditionally required months of specialized engineering. We present a reusable recipe - a generic prompt template, hierarchical verification, and iterative agent-assisted repair - that produces semantically equivalent high-performance environments for <$10 in compute cost. We demonstrate three distinct workflows across five environments. Direct translation (no prior performance implementation exists): EmuRust (1.5x PPO speedup via Rust parallelism for a Game Boy emulator) and PokeJAX, the first GPU-parallel Pokemon battle simulator (500M SPS random action, 15.2M SPS PPO; 22,320x over the TypeScript reference). Translation verified against existing performance implementations: throughput parity with MJX (1.04x) and 5x over Brax at matched GPU batch sizes (HalfCheetah JAX); 42x PPO (Puffer Pong). New environment creation: TCGJax, the first deployable JAX Pokemon TCG engine (717K SPS random action, 153K SPS PPO; 6.6x over the Python reference), synthesized from a web-extracted specification. At 200M parameters, the environment overhead drops below 4% of training time. Hierarchical verification (property, interaction, and rollout tests) confirms semantic equivalence for all five environments; cross-backend policy transfer confirms zero sim-to-sim gap for all five environments. TCGJax, synthesized from a private reference absent from public repositories, serves as a contamination control for agent pretraining data concerns. The paper contains sufficient detail - including representative prompts, verification methodology, and complete results - that a coding agent could reproduce the translations directly from the manuscript.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high engineering cost of creating high-performance reinforcement learning environments, the paper introduces a reusable recipe combining generic prompting, hierarchical verification, and agent-assisted iterative repair to generate semantically equivalent, high-speed environments for under $10 in compute. Applied across five environments, it achieves direct translation of Game Boy emulator (EmuRust, 1.5x PPO speedup) and GPU-parallel Pokemon battle simulator (PokeJAX, 22,320x faster than TypeScript), verifies parity with existing implementations (MJX, 5x Brax, 42x PPO), and synthesizes new environments like TCGJax (6.6x faster than Python) from web specifications. All environments pass hierarchical verification and show zero simulation-to-simulation gap across backends, with environment overhead under 4% at 200M parameters, and TCGJax serves as a contamination control for agent pretraining.
为降低构建高性能强化学习环境的工程成本，本文提出一套可复用的配方，结合通用提示模板、分层验证与智能体辅助迭代修复，自动生成语义等价的高性能环境，且计算成本低于10美元。在五个环境中应用该方法，实现了Game Boy模拟器的直接翻译（通过Rust并行获得1.5倍PPO加速）和首个GPU并行的宝可梦战斗模拟器（比TypeScript参考实现快22320倍），验证环境与现有实现相比吞吐量持平或最高提升5倍，同时从网络提取规范合成新环境TCGJax（比Python参考实现快6.6倍）。所有环境均通过分层验证，跨后端策略迁移无模拟器间性能差距，200M参数下环境开销低于训练时间的4%，且TCGJax作为私有参考环境，用于控制智能体预训练数据污染。
Estimating Canopy Height at Scale
Authors: Jan Pauls, Max Zimmer, Una M. Kelly, Martin Schwartz, Sassan Saatchi, Philippe Ciais, Sebastian Pokutta, Martin Brandt, Fabian Gieseke
Venue: ICML
First: 2024-06-03T07:53:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:44+00:00
Comments: ICML Camera-Ready, 17 pages, 14 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a framework for global-scale canopy height estimation based on satellite data. Our model leverages advanced data preprocessing techniques, resorts to a novel loss function designed to counter geolocation inaccuracies inherent in the ground-truth height measurements, and employs data from the Shuttle Radar Topography Mission to effectively filter out erroneous labels in mountainous regions, enhancing the reliability of our predictions in those areas. A comparison between predictions and ground-truth labels yields an MAE / RMSE of 2.43 / 4.73 (meters) overall and 4.45 / 6.72 (meters) for trees taller than five meters, which depicts a substantial improvement compared to existing global-scale maps. The resulting height map as well as the underlying framework will facilitate and enhance ecological analyses at a global scale, including, but not limited to, large-scale forest and biomass monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模冠层高度估算
我们提出了一种基于卫星数据的全球尺度冠层高度估算框架。该模型采用先进的数据预处理技术，引入一种新型损失函数以应对地面真实高度测量中存在的地理位置误差，并利用航天飞机雷达地形任务数据有效过滤山区的错误标签，从而提升这些区域预测结果的可靠性。预测结果与真实标签对比，整体MAE/RMSE为2.43/4.73米，树高超过五米的区域为4.45/6.72米，相较现有全球尺度地图有显著改进。所得高度图及底层框架将有助于并提升全球范围内的生态分析，包括但不限于大规模森林与生物量监测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate global canopy height data for ecological monitoring, this study proposes a satellite-based framework that uses advanced preprocessing, a novel loss function to mitigate geolocation errors in ground-truth data, and Shuttle Radar Topography Mission data to filter erroneous labels in mountainous regions. Experimental results show an overall MAE/RMSE of 2.43/4.73 meters and 4.45/6.72 meters for trees over five meters tall, significantly outperforming existing global maps and enabling improved large-scale forest and biomass analysis.
为支持全球生态监测，该研究提出一种基于卫星数据的树冠高度估算框架，通过先进预处理、专为解决地面真值定位误差设计的新损失函数，以及利用航天器雷达地形任务数据过滤山地错误标签，提升预测可靠性。实验结果显示，整体MAE/RMSE为2.43/4.73米，高于5米树木为4.45/6.72米，显著优于现有全球地图，有助于提升大尺度森林与生物量监测能力。
O3N: Omnidirectional Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction
Authors: Mengfei Duan, Hao Shi, Fei Teng, Guoqiang Zhao, Yuheng Zhang, Zhiyong Li, Kailun Yang
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:42+00:00
Comments: The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding and reconstructing the 3D world through omnidirectional perception is an inevitable trend in the development of autonomous agents and embodied intelligence. However, existing 3D occupancy prediction methods are constrained by limited perspective inputs and predefined training distribution, making them difficult to apply to embodied agents that require comprehensive and safe perception of scenes in open world exploration. To address this, we present O3N, the first purely visual, end-to-end Omnidirectional Open-vocabulary Occupancy predictioN framework. O3N embeds omnidirectional voxels in a polar-spiral topology via the Polar-spiral Mamba (PsM) module, enabling continuous spatial representation and long-range context modeling across 360°. The Occupancy Cost Aggregation (OCA) module introduces a principled mechanism for unifying geometric and semantic supervision within the voxel space, ensuring consistency between the reconstructed geometry and the underlying semantic structure. Moreover, Natural Modality Alignment (NMA) establishes a gradient-free alignment pathway that harmonizes visual features, voxel embeddings, and text semantics, forming a consistent "pixel-voxel-text" representation triad. Extensive experiments on multiple models demonstrate that our method not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on QuadOcc and Human360Occ benchmarks but also exhibits remarkable cross-scene generalization and semantic scalability, paving the way toward universal 3D world modeling. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N.
中文标题/摘要
标题：O3N：全方位开放词汇占用预测
通过全方位感知理解并重建三维世界，是自主代理和具身智能发展的必然趋势。然而，现有三维占用预测方法受限于有限视角输入和预定义训练分布，难以应用于需要在开放世界探索中全面、安全感知场景的具身代理。为此，我们提出了O3N，首个纯视觉、端到端的全方位开放词汇占用预测框架。O3N通过极螺旋Mamba（PsM）模块，将全方位体素嵌入极螺旋拓扑结构，实现连续空间表示与360°长程上下文建模。占用成本聚合（OCA）模块在体素空间内引入几何与语义监督统一的机制，确保重建几何与底层语义结构的一致性。此外，自然模态对齐（NMA）建立无梯度对齐路径，协调视觉特征、体素嵌入与文本语义，形成一致的“像素-体素-文本”三元表示。在多个模型上的大量实验表明，我们的方法不仅在QuadOcc和Human360Occ基准上达到最先进性能，还展现出卓越的跨场景泛化能力和语义可扩展性，为通用三维世界建模奠定基础。源代码将在https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N公开发布。
Summary / 总结
O3N addresses the limitations of existing 3D occupancy prediction methods by introducing a purely visual, end-to-end framework designed for omnidirectional, open-vocabulary scene reconstruction. It employs a Polar-spiral Mamba module to encode 360-degree spatial data into continuous voxel representations, enabling long-range context modeling. The Occupancy Cost Aggregation module unifies geometric and semantic supervision within voxel space, while Natural Modality Alignment establishes a gradient-free pathway to align visual, voxel, and textual features. Experiments on QuadOcc and Human360Occ benchmarks show O3N achieves state-of-the-art performance and demonstrates strong cross-scene generalization and semantic scalability, supporting broader applications in embodied AI.
为满足具身智能体在开放世界探索中对全面且安全场景感知的需求，O3N提出首个纯视觉端到端的全方位开放词汇占用预测框架。其采用极螺旋Mamba模块将360度全景体素嵌入极螺旋拓扑结构，实现连续空间表示与长程上下文建模。占用代价聚合模块统一几何与语义监督，自然模态对齐模块则无梯度地对齐视觉、体素与文本特征，构建一致的“像素-体素-文本”三元表示。在QuadOcc和Human360Occ数据集上，O3N达到最先进性能，并展现出优异的跨场景泛化能力与语义可扩展性。
HATS: Hardness-Aware Trajectory Synthesis for GUI Agents
Authors: Rui Shao, Ruize Gao, Bin Xie, Yixing Li, Kaiwen Zhou, Shuai Wang, Weili Guan, Gongwei Chen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:40:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphical user interface (GUI) agents powered by large vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable potential in automating digital tasks, highlighting the need for high-quality trajectory data to support effective agent training. Yet existing trajectory synthesis pipelines often yield agents that fail to generalize beyond simple interactions. We identify this limitation as stemming from the neglect of semantically ambiguous actions, whose meanings are context-dependent, sequentially dependent, or visually ambiguous. Such actions are crucial for real-world robustness but are under-represented and poorly processed in current datasets, leading to semantic misalignment between task instructions and execution. To address these issues, we propose HATS, a Hardness-Aware Trajectory Synthesis framework designed to mitigate the impact of semantic ambiguity. We define hardness as the degree of semantic ambiguity associated with an action and develop two complementary modules: (1) hardness-driven exploration, which guides data collection toward ambiguous yet informative interactions, and (2) alignment-guided refinement, which iteratively validates and repairs instruction-execution alignment. The two modules operate in a closed loop: exploration supplies refinement with challenging trajectories, while refinement feedback updates the hardness signal to guide future exploration. Extensive experiments show that agents trained with HATS consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines across benchmark GUI environments.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of GUI agents trained on existing trajectory data, this work addresses the underrepresentation of semantically ambiguous actions that are critical for real-world robustness. HATS introduces a Hardness-Aware Trajectory Synthesis framework that defines action hardness as semantic ambiguity and employs two complementary modules: hardness-driven exploration to collect challenging yet informative interactions, and alignment-guided refinement to iteratively validate and correct instruction-execution mismatches. These modules operate in a closed loop, where refinement feedback updates the hardness signal to steer future exploration. Experimental results demonstrate that agents trained using HATS consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines across multiple GUI benchmarks.
为解决现有GUI代理在复杂交互中泛化能力差的问题，本文提出HATS框架，通过引入动作语义模糊度（hardness）概念提升训练数据质量。该框架包含两个模块：硬度驱动探索模块主动采集高模糊度但信息丰富的交互轨迹，对齐引导优化模块则迭代修正指令与执行之间的语义偏差。两模块形成闭环反馈机制，优化结果用于动态调整模糊度信号以指导后续探索。实验表明，使用HATS训练的代理在多个GUI基准测试中持续优于当前最优方法。
Multi-lingual Functional Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Victor Ojewale, Inioluwa Deborah Raji, Suresh Venkatasubramanian
First: 2025-06-25T19:32:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:38:30+00:00
Comments: This is an updated version with details of the CL-GSM Symbolic and CL-IFEval datasets validation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-lingual competence in large language models is often evaluated via static data benchmarks such as Belebele, M-MMLU and M-GSM. However, these evaluations often fail to provide an adequate understanding of the practical performance and robustness of models across multi-lingual settings. In response, we create multi-lingual functional benchmarks -- Cross-Lingual Grade School Math Symbolic (CL-GSM Symbolic) and Cross-Lingual Instruction-Following Eval (CL-IFEval)-- by translating existing functional benchmark templates from English to five additional languages that span the range of resources available for NLP: French, Spanish, Hindi, Arabic and Yoruba. Our results reveal that some static multi-lingual benchmarks capture functional performance much more closely than others (i.e. across models, there is a 24%, 17% and 18% decrease in performance between M-GSM and CL-GSM Symbolic in English, French and Spanish respectively; similarly there's a 15 - 24% performance drop across languages between Belebele and CL-IFEval, and only a 0.5% to 3% performance drop between M-MMLU and CL-IFEval). Similarly, we find that model robustness across languages varies significantly, with certain languages (eg. Arabic, English) being the most consistently well performing across evaluation iterations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型多语言功能评估
大语言模型的多语言能力常通过静态数据基准（如Belebele、M-MMLU和M-GSM）进行评估。然而，这些评估往往无法充分反映模型在多语言环境中的实际表现与鲁棒性。为此，我们创建了两个多语言功能基准——跨语言小学数学符号基准（CL-GSM Symbolic）和跨语言指令遵循评估（CL-IFEval），通过将现有功能基准模板从英语翻译至五种资源覆盖范围不同的语言：法语、西班牙语、印地语、阿拉伯语和约鲁巴语。结果表明，部分静态多语言基准比其他基准更贴近功能表现（例如，在英语、法语和西班牙语中，M-GSM与CL-GSM Symbolic的性能分别下降24%、17%和18%；同样，Belebele与CL-IFEval在各语言间的性能下降为15%-24%，而M-MMLU与CL-IFEval之间仅下降0.5%-3%）。此外，我们发现模型在不同语言间的鲁棒性差异显著，某些语言（如阿拉伯语、英语）在多次评估中表现最为稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static multi-lingual benchmarks in assessing real-world model performance, this study introduces two new functional benchmarks—CL-GSM Symbolic and CL-IFEval—translated into five low-to-medium resource languages (French, Spanish, Hindi, Arabic, Yoruba) to better evaluate cross-lingual capabilities. The benchmarks are designed to test symbolic math reasoning and instruction-following in context, respectively. Experimental results show that CL-GSM Symbolic outperforms static benchmarks like M-GSM by 24% in English, 17% in French, and 18% in Spanish, while CL-IFEval shows a 15–24% performance gap compared to Belebele but only a 0.5–3% gap relative to M-MMLU. Additionally, model robustness varies significantly across languages, with Arabic and English consistently performing best across evaluation iterations.
为弥补静态多语言基准在评估模型实际能力方面的不足，作者构建了两个新的功能性基准——CL-GSM Symbolic 和 CL-IFEval，通过将英文模板翻译成法语、西班牙语、印地语、阿拉伯语和约鲁巴语五种资源较少的语言。实验结果表明，CL-GSM Symbolic 在英语、法语和西班牙语中分别比 M-GSM 提高了 24%、17% 和 18%；CL-IFEval 使 Belebele 与 M-MMLU 之间的性能差距缩小至 15–24% 和 0.5–3%。同时，研究发现模型在不同语言间的鲁棒性差异显著，阿拉伯语和英语在多次评估中表现最为稳定。
TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning
Authors: Mayug Maniparambil, Nils Hoehing, Janak Kapuriya, Arjun Karuvally, Ellen Rushe, Anthony Ventresque, Noel O'Connor, Fergal Reid
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted, Workshop on Logical Reasoning of Large Language Models at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in solving topological grid puzzles that require global spatial reasoning, the authors introduce TopoBench, a benchmark with six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. They evaluate top-tier LLMs and find that even the most capable models solve fewer than 25% of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolvable. To diagnose failure causes, they annotate 750 chain-of-thought traces with an error taxonomy, identifying four failure modes, and test them via targeted interventions. Results show that premature commitment and constraint forgetting significantly impair performance, while repeated reasoning is harmless. Mitigation strategies like prompt guidance and grid-aligned representations improve results, but the core bottleneck remains extracting spatial constraints, not reasoning over them.
为研究大语言模型在需要全局空间推理的拓扑网格谜题上的能力，作者构建了TopoBench基准，包含六个谜题家族和三个难度等级。实验发现，即使前沿模型在高难度谜题上的正确率也低于25%，其中两个家族几乎无法解决。通过标注750条思维链并建立错误分类体系，作者识别出四种失败模式，并通过针对性干预验证其影响，发现过早承诺和约束遗忘显著降低解题能力，而重复推理无害。缓解策略如提示引导和网格表示虽有提升，但核心瓶颈仍在于从空间表示中提取约束，而非推理过程本身。
Lifelong Imitation Learning with Multimodal Latent Replay and Incremental Adjustment
Authors: Fanqi Yu, Matteo Tiezzi, Tommaso Apicella, Cigdem Beyan, Vittorio Murino
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-11T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:36:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce a lifelong imitation learning framework that enables continual policy refinement across sequential tasks under realistic memory and data constraints. Our approach departs from conventional experience replay by operating entirely in a multimodal latent space, where compact representations of visual, linguistic, and robot's state information are stored and reused to support future learning. To further stabilize adaptation, we introduce an incremental feature adjustment mechanism that regularizes the evolution of task embeddings through an angular margin constraint, preserving inter-task distinctiveness. Our method establishes a new state of the art in the LIBERO benchmarks, achieving 10-17 point gains in AUC and up to 65% less forgetting compared to previous leading methods. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component, showing consistent gains over alternative strategies. The code is available at: https://github.com/yfqi/lifelong_mlr_ifa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态潜在重放与增量调整的终身模仿学习
我们提出了一种终身模仿学习框架，可在现实的记忆和数据约束下，持续优化跨序列任务的策略。该方法不同于传统经验回放，完全在多模态潜在空间中运作，其中存储并重用视觉、语言及机器人状态信息的紧凑表示，以支持未来学习。为进一步稳定适应，我们引入增量特征调整机制，通过角度间隔约束正则化任务嵌入的演化，保持任务间差异性。该方法在LIBERO基准上达到新状态，AUC提升10-17分，遗忘率比先前领先方法低65%。消融实验验证各组件有效性，表现优于替代策略。代码见：https://github.com/yfqi/lifelong_mlr_ifa。
Summary / 总结
We introduce a lifelong imitation learning framework that enables continual policy refinement across sequential tasks under realistic memory and data constraints.
为了解决在记忆与数据受限条件下持续改进策略的需求，该研究提出一种在多模态潜在空间中操作的框架，存储视觉、语言和机器人状态的紧凑表示以供复用。为稳定跨任务适应，引入增量特征调整机制，通过角度边际约束调节任务嵌入的演化，保持任务间差异性。在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法相比先前最先进方法在AUC上提升10-17分，遗忘率降低高达65%，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
On the Theoretical Limitations of Embedding-Based Retrieval
Authors: Orion Weller, Michael Boratko, Iftekhar Naim, Jinhyuk Lee
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-08-28T17:43:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:35:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector embeddings have been tasked with an ever-increasing set of retrieval tasks over the years, with a nascent rise in using them for reasoning, instruction-following, coding, and more. These new benchmarks push embeddings to work for any query and any notion of relevance that could be given. While prior works have pointed out theoretical limitations of vector embeddings, there is a common assumption that these difficulties are exclusively due to unrealistic queries, and those that are not can be overcome with better training data and larger models. In this work, we demonstrate that we may encounter these theoretical limitations in realistic settings with extremely simple queries. We connect known results in learning theory, showing that the number of top-k subsets of documents capable of being returned as the result of some query is limited by the dimension of the embedding. We empirically show that this holds true even if we directly optimize on the test set with free parameterized embeddings. Using free embeddings, we then demonstrate that returning all pairs of documents requires a relatively high dimension. We then create a realistic dataset called LIMIT that stress tests embedding models based on these theoretical results, and observe that even state-of-the-art models fail on this dataset despite the simple nature of the task. Our work shows the limits of embedding models under the existing single vector paradigm and calls for future research to develop new techniques that can resolve this fundamental limitation.
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the theoretical constraints of embedding-based retrieval systems, challenging the belief that limitations stem only from unrealistic queries. It connects learning theory to show that the number of top-k document subsets retrievable via vector embeddings is fundamentally bounded by embedding dimension, regardless of training data or model size. Empirical experiments using free-parameterized embeddings confirm this bound, even when directly optimizing on test sets, and demonstrate that returning all document pairs requires high dimensionality. The authors introduce LIMIT, a realistic dataset designed to stress-test embeddings based on these theoretical limits, and find that state-of-the-art models fail despite simple queries, revealing inherent limitations of the single-vector paradigm.
本文研究了基于嵌入的检索系统的理论限制，挑战了认为这些限制仅源于不现实查询的普遍观点。通过连接学习理论，作者证明了可被查询返回的top-k文档子集数量受嵌入维度限制，无论训练数据或模型规模如何。实验表明，即使直接在测试集上优化自由参数化嵌入，该限制依然成立，且返回所有文档对需要高维嵌入。作者构建了名为LIMIT的真实数据集，基于理论结果对嵌入模型进行压力测试，发现即使查询简单，当前最先进模型仍失败，揭示了单向量范式固有的局限性。
Increasing intelligence in AI agents can worsen collective outcomes
Authors: Neil F. Johnson
First: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When resources are scarce, will a population of AI agents coordinate in harmony, or descend into tribal chaos? Diverse decision-making AI from different developers is entering everyday devices -- from phones and medical devices to battlefield drones and cars -- and these AI agents typically compete for finite shared resources such as charging slots, relay bandwidth, and traffic priority. Yet their collective dynamics and hence risks to users and society are poorly understood. Here we study AI-agent populations as the first system of real agents in which four key variables governing collective behaviour can be independently toggled: nature (innate LLM diversity), nurture (individual reinforcement learning), culture (emergent tribe formation), and resource scarcity. We show empirically and mathematically that when resources are scarce, AI model diversity and reinforcement learning increase dangerous system overload, though tribe formation lessens this risk. Meanwhile, some individuals profit handsomely. When resources are abundant, the same ingredients drive overload to near zero, though tribe formation makes the overload slightly worse. The crossover is arithmetical: it is where opposing tribes that form spontaneously first fit inside the available capacity. More sophisticated AI-agent populations are not better: whether their sophistication helps or harms depends entirely on a single number -- the capacity-to-population ratio -- that is knowable before any AI-agent ships.
中文标题/摘要
标题：增强AI代理的智能可能恶化集体结果
当资源稀缺时，AI代理群体是和谐协作还是陷入部落式混乱？来自不同开发者的多样化决策AI正进入日常设备——从手机和医疗设备到战场无人机和汽车——这些AI代理通常竞争有限的共享资源，如充电插口、中继带宽和交通优先权。然而，它们的集体动态及其对用户和社会的风险尚不明确。本文首次将AI代理群体作为可独立调节四大关键变量的系统进行研究：本性（固有LLM多样性）、养育（个体强化学习）、文化（自发部落形成）和资源稀缺性。我们通过实证和数学方法表明，当资源稀缺时，AI模型多样性与强化学习会加剧系统危险过载，但部落形成可降低此风险；同时部分个体获利颇丰。当资源充足时，相同因素使过载接近零，但部落形成反而略微加剧过载。转折点是算术性的：即自发形成的对立部落首次能容纳于可用容量之内。更复杂的AI代理群体并不更优：其智能是否有益或有害，完全取决于一个可预先知晓的单一数值——容量与群体规模之比。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how diverse AI agents interact under resource constraints, this study investigates collective behavior by independently varying four factors: innate model diversity, individual reinforcement learning, emergent tribe formation, and resource scarcity. Experiments reveal that under scarce resources, increased AI sophistication and diversity lead to dangerous system overload, while tribe formation mitigates this risk, even as some agents benefit disproportionately. When resources are abundant, the same factors cause negligible overload, though tribes slightly worsen it. The critical tipping point is arithmetic: it occurs when opposing tribes first fit within available capacity, and whether AI sophistication helps or harms depends entirely on the capacity-to-population ratio, a measurable metric before deployment.
为理解不同AI代理在资源受限条件下如何协作或冲突，本研究通过独立调节四个关键变量——模型多样性、个体强化学习、部落形成和资源稀缺性——来研究AI代理群体的集体行为。实验表明，在资源稀缺时，AI能力越强、多样性越高，系统越易过载，而部落形成可缓解此风险，同时部分个体获益显著；在资源充足时，这些因素几乎消除过载，但部落形成反而略微加剧过载。关键转折点是纯数学关系：当群体规模超过系统容量时，部落自发形成并引发过载，无论AI能力高低。
Hoi3DGen: Generating High-Quality Human-Object-Interactions in 3D
Authors: Agniv Sharma, Xianghui Xie, Tom Fischer, Eddy Ilg, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling and generating 3D human-object interactions from text is crucial for applications in AR, XR, and gaming. Existing approaches often rely on score distillation from text-to-image models, but their results suffer from the Janus problem and do not follow text prompts faithfully due to the scarcity of high-quality interaction data. We introduce Hoi3DGen, a framework that generates high-quality textured meshes of human-object interaction that follow the input interaction descriptions precisely. We first curate realistic and high-quality interaction data leveraging multimodal large language models, and then create a full text-to-3D pipeline, which achieves orders-of-magnitude improvements in interaction fidelity. Our method surpasses baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, exhibiting strong generalization to diverse categories and interaction types, while maintaining high-quality 3D generation.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate 3D human-object interaction generation for AR, XR, and gaming, Hoi3DGen addresses the limitations of existing text-to-image-based methods that suffer from poor fidelity and prompt misalignment due to scarce high-quality data. The method first constructs a curated dataset of realistic interactions using multimodal large language models, then builds a full text-to-3D pipeline that directly generates textured 3D meshes. Experimental results show Hoi3DGen outperforms baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, with strong generalization across diverse interaction types and categories.
为满足AR、XR和游戏领域对高质量3D人机交互生成的需求，Hoi3DGen针对现有基于文本到图像方法存在的文本忠实度低和Janus问题，提出直接生成纹理化3D网格的完整文本到3D流程。该方法首先利用多模态大语言模型构建高质量交互数据集，再通过端到端生成流程输出精确匹配文本描述的3D模型。实验表明，该方法在文本一致性上比基线高4-15倍，3D模型质量提升3-7倍，且在多种交互类型和类别上具备良好泛化能力。
WideSeek-R1: Exploring Width Scaling for Broad Information Seeking via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zelai Xu, Zhexuan Xu, Ruize Zhang, Chunyang Zhu, Shi Yu, Weilin Liu, Quanlu Zhang, Wenbo Ding, Chao Yu, Yu Wang
First: 2026-02-04T15:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:55+00:00
Comments: https://wideseek-r1.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have largely focused on depth scaling, where a single agent solves long-horizon problems with multi-turn reasoning and tool use. However, as tasks grow broader, the key bottleneck shifts from individual competence to organizational capability. In this work, we explore a complementary dimension of width scaling with multi-agent systems to address broad information seeking. Existing multi-agent systems often rely on hand-crafted workflows and turn-taking interactions that fail to parallelize work effectively. To bridge this gap, we propose WideSeek-R1, a lead-agent-subagent framework trained via multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) to synergize scalable orchestration and parallel execution. By utilizing a shared LLM with isolated contexts and specialized tools, WideSeek-R1 jointly optimizes the lead agent and parallel subagents on a curated dataset of 20k broad information-seeking tasks. Extensive experiments show that WideSeek-R1-4B achieves an item F1 score of 40.0% on the WideSearch benchmark, which is comparable to the performance of single-agent DeepSeek-R1-671B. Furthermore, WideSeek-R1-4B exhibits consistent performance gains as the number of parallel subagents increases, highlighting the effectiveness of width scaling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WideSeek-R1：通过多智能体强化学习探索宽度扩展以应对广泛信息检索
近期大语言模型（LLMs）的进展主要集中在深度扩展，即单个智能体通过多轮推理和工具使用解决长周期问题。然而，随着任务范围扩大，关键瓶颈从个体能力转向组织能力。本研究探索了通过多智能体系统实现宽度扩展的互补维度，以应对广泛信息检索。现有多智能体系统常依赖人工编写的流程和轮流交互，难以有效并行工作。为弥合这一差距，我们提出WideSeek-R1，一种通过多智能体强化学习（MARL）训练的主智能体-子智能体框架，协同实现可扩展编排与并行执行。该框架利用共享LLM、隔离上下文和专用工具，在2万条广泛信息检索任务的精选数据集上联合优化主智能体与并行子智能体。大量实验表明，WideSeek-R1-4B在WideSearch基准测试中达到40.0%的项目F1分数，与单智能体DeepSeek-R1-671B表现相当。此外，随着并行子智能体数量增加，WideSeek-R1-4B持续表现出性能提升，凸显了宽度扩展的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of depth scaling in large language models for broad information seeking, this work introduces WideSeek-R1, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that scales via width rather than depth. It employs a lead-agent-subagent architecture with shared LLMs and isolated contexts to enable parallel subagent execution, overcoming inefficiencies in existing hand-crafted multi-agent workflows. Experiments on 20k broad information-seeking tasks show that WideSeek-R1-4B achieves 40.0% item F1 on the WideSearch benchmark, matching the performance of the much larger DeepSeek-R1-671B, and demonstrates consistent performance gains as more parallel subagents are added.
为应对大语言模型在广域信息检索任务中深度扩展的局限性，本研究提出WideSeek-R1多智能体强化学习框架，通过宽度扩展而非深度扩展提升性能。该框架采用主智能体-子智能体架构，共享LLM并隔离上下文，支持子智能体并行执行，克服现有手工设计多智能体流程的效率瓶颈。在2万条广域信息检索任务上实验表明，WideSeek-R1-4B在WideSearch基准测试中达到40.0%的项目F1分数，与参数规模大得多的DeepSeek-R1-671B表现相当，且随并行子智能体数量增加持续提升性能。
Cross-Context Review: Improving LLM Output Quality by Separating Production and Review Sessions
Authors: Tae-Eun Song
First: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models struggle to catch errors in their own outputs when the review happens in the same session that produced them. This paper introduces Cross-Context Review (CCR), a straightforward method where the review is conducted in a fresh session with no access to the production conversation history. We ran a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts (code, technical documents, presentation scripts) with 150 injected errors, tested under four review conditions -- same-session Self-Review (SR), repeated Self-Review (SR2), context-aware Subagent Review (SA), and Cross-Context Review (CCR). Over 360 reviews, CCR reached an F1 of 28.6%, outperforming SR (24.6%, p=0.008, d=0.52), SR2 (21.7%, p<0.001, d=0.72), and SA (23.8%, p=0.004, d=0.57). The SR2 result matters most for interpretation: reviewing twice in the same session did not beat reviewing once (p=0.11), which rules out repetition as an explanation for CCR's advantage. The benefit comes from context separation itself. CCR works with any model, needs no infrastructure, and costs only one extra session.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨上下文审查：通过分离生成与审查会话提升大语言模型输出质量
大语言模型在自身输出的同一会话中进行审查时难以发现错误。本文提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，即在无生成对话历史的新会话中进行审查。我们进行了受控实验：30个工件（代码、技术文档、演示脚本），共150个注入错误，在四种审查条件下测试——同会话自审查（SR）、重复自审查（SR2）、上下文感知子代理审查（SA）和跨上下文审查（CCR）。在360次审查中，CCR达到F1值28.6%，优于SR（24.6%，p=0.008，d=0.52）、SR2（21.7%，p<0.001，d=0.72）和SA（23.8%，p=0.004，d=0.57）。SR2结果对解释至关重要：同会话内重复审查并未优于单次审查（p=0.11），排除重复作为CCR优势的解释。优势源于上下文分离本身。CCR适用于任何模型，无需基础设施，仅需额外一个会话。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of large language models in self-correcting their own outputs when review occurs within the same session as production. It proposes Cross-Context Review (CCR), a method that isolates review into a fresh session with no access to prior production context. In a controlled experiment with 30 artifacts containing 150 injected errors, CCR achieved an F1 score of 28.6%, significantly outperforming same-session self-review (24.6%), repeated self-review (21.7%), and context-aware subagent review (23.8%). The lack of improvement from repeated self-review (p=0.11) suggests that context separation—not repetition—is key to CCR’s advantage. The method is model-agnostic, requires no infrastructure, and incurs only one additional session cost.
该论文针对大语言模型在生成后同一会话内自我纠错效果不佳的问题，提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，即在独立的新会话中进行审查，且不访问生成过程中的历史上下文。实验中对30个包含150处注入错误的工件进行测试，CCR以28.6%的F1分数显著优于同会话自审（24.6%）、重复自审（21.7%）和上下文感知子代理审查（23.8%）。重复自审无显著提升（p=0.11），表明CCR的优势源于上下文分离而非重复。该方法无需额外基础设施，兼容任何模型，仅需增加一个会话成本。
From Toil to Thought: Designing for Strategic Exploration and Responsible AI in Systematic Literature Reviews
Authors: Runlong Ye, Naaz Sibia, Angela Zavaleta Bernuy, Tingting Zhu, Carolina Nobre, Viktoria Pammer-Schindler, Michael Liut
First: 2026-01-21T00:28:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:25:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IUI 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs) are fundamental to scientific progress, yet the process is hindered by a fragmented tool ecosystem that imposes a high cognitive load. This friction suppresses the iterative, exploratory nature of scholarly work. To investigate these challenges, we conducted an exploratory design study with 20 experienced researchers. This study identified key friction points: 1) the high cognitive load of managing iterative query refinement across multiple databases, 2) the overwhelming scale and pace of publication of modern literature, and 3) the tension between automation and scholarly agency. Informed by these findings, we developed ARC, a design probe that operationalizes solutions for multi-database integration, transparent iterative search, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers suggests that an integrated environment facilitates a transition in scholarly work, moving researchers from managing administrative overhead to engaging in strategic exploration. By utilizing external representations to scaffold strategic exploration and transparent AI reasoning, our system supports verifiable judgment, aiming to augment expert contributions from initial creation through long-term maintenance of knowledge synthesis.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the cognitive burden and fragmentation in conducting Systematic Literature Reviews, this study engaged 20 experienced researchers to identify key friction points, including multi-database query management, overwhelming publication volume, and tension between automation and scholarly agency. Based on these insights, the researchers designed ARC, a design probe that integrates multi-database search, transparent iterative querying, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers demonstrated that ARC reduces administrative overhead and enables researchers to shift toward strategic exploration, supported by external representations and transparent AI reasoning, thereby enhancing verifiable judgment throughout knowledge synthesis.
为解决系统性文献综述中认知负荷高、工具碎片化及自动化与学者自主性冲突等问题，研究者对20名经验丰富的研究人员进行探索性设计研究，识别出多数据库查询管理、文献规模过载及自动化与学术自主性张力三大痛点。基于此，研究者设计了ARC原型，实现多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索与可验证的AI辅助筛选功能。对8名研究人员的对比实验表明，ARC有效降低行政负担，使学者能转向战略探索，借助外部表征与透明AI推理支持可验证判断，从而提升知识综合全过程的专业贡献。
CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance
Authors: Leo Lin, Shivansh Patel, Jay Moon, Svetlana Lazebnik, Unnat Jain
First: 2026-03-12T16:23:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:23:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for hands capable of robust yet gentle manipulation in contact-rich environments, CRAFT introduces a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance, placing soft materials at joints to absorb impact while keeping finger links rigid for load-bearing. The design employs rolling-contact joint surfaces to ensure repeatable flexion and uses 15 finger-mounted motors to drive motion through tendons, enabling a compact and lightweight structure. Experimental results show improved structural strength and endurance compared to conventional designs, while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation tasks, CRAFT demonstrates superior handling of fragile and low-friction objects, achieving all 33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy, and the entire system costs under $600.
为实现接触密集环境下兼具强度与柔韧性的操作，CRAFT手采用硬软混合结构，关节处使用软材料缓冲冲击，指节保持刚性以承载负载，并通过滚动接触关节表面确保运动重复性。15个电机经肌腱驱动手指，实现紧凑轻量结构。实验表明其结构强度与耐久性优于传统设计，遥操作测试中对易碎及低摩擦物体的处理能力更优，可完成Feix分类中的全部33种抓取动作。整套设计成本低于600美元，开源并支持基于视觉的遥操作与仿真集成。
RefTr: Recurrent Refinement of Confluent Trajectories for 3D Vascular Tree Centerlines
Authors: Roman Naeem, David Hagerman, Jennifer Alvén, Fredrik Kahl
First: 2025-11-25T20:22:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:22:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tubular tree structures such as blood vessels and lung airways are central to many clinical tasks, including diagnosis, treatment planning, and surgical navigation. Accurate centerline extraction with correct topology is essential, as missing small branches can lead to incomplete assessments or overlooked abnormalities. We propose RefTr, a 3D image-to-graph framework that generates vascular centerlines via recurrent refinement of confluent trajectories. RefTr adopts a Transformer-based Producer-Refiner architecture in which the Producer predicts candidate trajectories and a shared Refiner iteratively refines them toward the target branches. The confluent trajectory representation enables whole-branch refinement while explicitly enforcing valid topology. This recurrent scheme improves precision and reduces decoder parameters by 2.4x compared to the state-of-the-art. We further introduce an efficient non-maximum suppression algorithm for spatial tree graphs to merge duplicate branches and extend evaluation metrics to be radius-aware for robust comparison. Experiments on multiple public datasets demonstrate stronger overall performance, faster inference, and substantially fewer parameters, highlighting the effectiveness of RefTr for 3D vascular tree analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RefTr：用于3D血管树中心线的汇合轨迹递归精炼
血管和肺气道等管状树状结构在许多临床任务中至关重要，包括诊断、治疗规划和手术导航。准确提取具有正确拓扑结构的中心线至关重要，因为遗漏小分支可能导致评估不完整或忽略异常。我们提出RefTr，一种3D图像到图的框架，通过汇合轨迹的递归精炼生成血管中心线。RefTr采用基于Transformer的生产者-精炼者架构，其中生产者预测候选轨迹，共享精炼者迭代地将其精炼至目标分支。汇合轨迹表示支持整枝精炼，同时显式强制有效拓扑。该递归方案相比当前最优方法提升了精度，且解码器参数减少2.4倍。我们进一步引入一种高效的非极大值抑制算法，用于空间树图以合并重复分支，并将评估指标扩展为半径感知，以实现稳健比较。在多个公开数据集上的实验表明，RefTr在整体性能、推理速度和参数数量上均显著优于现有方法，凸显其在3D血管树分析中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and topologically correct centerline extraction in 3D vascular trees for clinical applications, RefTr introduces a 3D image-to-graph framework that uses recurrent refinement of confluent trajectories. It employs a Transformer-based Producer-Refiner architecture: the Producer generates candidate trajectories, and a shared Refiner iteratively refines them toward target branches while preserving valid topology. Experimental results on multiple public datasets show RefTr achieves superior performance, faster inference, and 2.4x fewer decoder parameters than the state-of-the-art, with an efficient non-maximum suppression algorithm and radius-aware metrics enhancing robustness and efficiency.
为满足临床任务中对3D血管树中心线精确提取与拓扑正确性的需求，RefTr提出一种基于递归优化的汇合轨迹框架。其采用Transformer架构的生产者-优化器结构：生产者生成候选轨迹，共享优化器迭代优化至目标分支并显式保证拓扑有效性。实验表明，RefTr在多个公开数据集上表现更优、推理更快、参数量减少2.4倍，同时引入高效的非极大值抑制算法与半径感知评估指标，显著提升鲁棒性与效率。
Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models
Authors: Jae-Won Chung, Jeff J. Ma, Jisang Ahn, Yizhuo Liang, Akshay Jajoo, Myungjin Lee, Mosharaf Chowdhury
Venue: www
First: 2026-03-12T16:20:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:20:35+00:00
Comments: Open source https://github.com/cornserve-ai/cornserve / Demo video https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nb8R-vztLRg
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics. We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cornserve：面向任意到任意多模态模型的分布式服务系统
任意到任意模型是新兴的多模态模型类别，可接受多种模态数据（如文本、图像、视频、音频）的组合作为输入，并生成相应模态的输出。服务此类模型具有挑战性：不同请求因输入和输出模态不同，需通过模型计算图的不同路径，且模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性。 我们提出Cornserve，一个通用任意到任意模型的分布式服务系统。Cornserve提供灵活的任务抽象，用于表达任意到任意模型的计算图，支持组件解耦与独立扩展。分布式运行时通过高效的记录-重放执行模型将计算调度至数据平面，跟踪数据依赖关系，并直接从生产者向消费者转发张量数据。基于Kubernetes构建，约23K行Python代码，Cornserve支持多种任意到任意模型，实现最高3.81倍吞吐量和5.79倍更低尾延迟。Cornserve开源，演示视频可在YouTube观看。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of serving Any-to-Any multimodal models that handle diverse input-output modalities with varying computational scaling, Cornserve introduces a distributed serving system that abstracts model computation graphs into flexible tasks, enabling independent scaling of components. It employs a record-and-replay execution model to efficiently dispatch compute via the data plane, forwarding tensor data directly between components based on dependencies. Experimental results show Cornserve achieves up to 3.81x higher throughput and 5.79x lower tail latency compared to baseline systems, while supporting diverse Any-to-Any models on Kubernetes.
为应对Any-to-Any多模态模型因输入输出模态多样且计算特性各异而带来的服务挑战，Cornserve提出一种分布式服务系统，通过灵活的任务抽象表达模型计算图，支持组件解耦与独立扩展。系统采用记录重放执行模型，通过数据平面高效调度计算，并直接在组件间传递张量数据以追踪依赖关系。实验表明，Cornserve相较基线系统吞吐量提升最高达3.81倍，尾延迟降低最高达5.79倍，且支持多种Any-to-Any模型在Kubernetes上运行。
On the Reliability of Cue Conflict and Beyond
Authors: Pum Jun Kim, Seung-Ah Lee, Seongho Park, Dongyoon Han, Jaejun Yoo
First: 2026-03-11T14:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:19:56+00:00
Comments: Shape-Texture Bias, Cue Conflict Benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how neural networks rely on visual cues offers a human-interpretable view of their internal decision processes. The cue-conflict benchmark has been influential in probing shape-texture preference and in motivating the insight that stronger, human-like shape bias is often associated with improved in-domain performance. However, we find that the current stylization-based instantiation can yield unstable and ambiguous bias estimates. Specifically, stylization may not reliably instantiate perceptually valid and separable cues nor control their relative informativeness, ratio-based bias can obscure absolute cue sensitivity, and restricting evaluation to preselected classes can distort model predictions by ignoring the full decision space. Together, these factors can confound preference with cue validity, cue balance, and recognizability artifacts. We introduce REFINED-BIAS, an integrated dataset and evaluation framework for reliable and interpretable shape-texture bias diagnosis. REFINED-BIAS constructs balanced, human- and model- recognizable cue pairs using explicit definitions of shape and texture, and measures cue-specific sensitivity over the full label space via a ranking-based metric, enabling fairer cross-model comparisons. Across diverse training regimes and architectures, REFINED-BIAS enables fairer cross-model comparison, more faithful diagnosis of shape and texture biases, and clearer empirical conclusions, resolving inconsistencies that prior cue-conflict evaluations could not reliably disambiguate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于线索冲突的可靠性及其超越
理解神经网络如何依赖视觉线索，可提供人类可解释的内部决策过程视角。线索冲突基准在探究形状-纹理偏好方面具有重要影响，并促使人们认识到更强的人类类形状偏见通常与更好的领域内性能相关。然而，我们发现当前基于风格化的方法可能导致不稳定且模糊的偏见估计。具体而言，风格化可能无法可靠地实例化感知上有效且可分离的线索，也无法控制其相对信息量；基于比率的偏见可能掩盖绝对线索敏感性；将评估限制在预选类别内可能通过忽略完整决策空间而扭曲模型预测。这些因素共同可能导致偏好与线索有效性、线索平衡及可识别性伪影混淆。我们提出了REFINED-BIAS，一个用于可靠且可解释的形状-纹理偏见诊断的集成数据集与评估框架。REFINED-BIAS通过明确的形状与纹理定义构建平衡且人类与模型均可识别的线索对，并通过基于排序的指标在完整标签空间上测量线索特异性敏感性，从而实现更公平的跨模型比较。在多样化的训练策略与架构下，REFINED-BIAS实现了更公平的跨模型比较、更准确的形状与纹理偏见诊断，以及更清晰的实证结论，解决了先前线索冲突评估无法可靠区分的不一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable and interpretable analysis of shape-texture bias in neural networks, this work critiques the limitations of existing stylization-based cue conflict benchmarks, which produce unstable bias estimates due to perceptual ambiguity, uncontrolled cue informativeness, ratio-based metrics, and restricted label spaces. To address these issues, the authors introduce REFINED-BIAS, a new dataset and evaluation framework that constructs balanced, human- and model-recognizable shape-texture cue pairs with explicit definitions, and measures cue-specific sensitivity across the full label space using a ranking-based metric. Experimental results across diverse architectures and training regimes show that REFINED-BIAS enables more accurate, fair, and consistent cross-model comparisons, resolves prior ambiguities in bias attribution, and provides clearer empirical insights into shape vs. texture preference.
本文针对现有视觉线索冲突基准在评估神经网络形状-纹理偏倚时存在的不稳定与模糊问题，指出其因风格化处理不当、基于比率的偏倚度量及预选类别限制，导致偏倚估计不可靠。作者提出REFINED-BIAS数据集与评估框架，通过明确定义形状与纹理并构建人与模型均可识别的平衡线索对，结合全标签空间的排序指标衡量各线索的敏感度。实验表明，该框架在多种模型架构与训练设置下，能实现更准确、公平、可解释的形状与纹理偏倚比较，有效解决以往基准的混淆问题，为模型决策机制提供更清晰的实证依据。
SommBench: Assessing Sommelier Expertise of Language Models
Authors: William Brach, Tomas Bedej, Jacob Nielsen, Jacob Pichna, Juraj Bedej, Eemeli Saarensilta, Julie Dupouy, Gianluca Barmina, Andrea Blasi Núñez, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Kristian Košťál, Michal Ries, Lukas Galke Poech
First: 2026-03-12T16:19:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:19:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
With the rapid advances of large language models, it becomes increasingly important to systematically evaluate their multilingual and multicultural capabilities. Previous cultural evaluation benchmarks focus mainly on basic cultural knowledge that can be encoded in linguistic form. Here, we propose SommBench, a multilingual benchmark to assess sommelier expertise, a domain deeply grounded in the senses of smell and taste. While language models learn about sensory properties exclusively through textual descriptions, SommBench tests whether this textual grounding is sufficient to emulate expert-level sensory judgment. SommBench comprises three main tasks: Wine Theory Question Answering (WTQA), Wine Feature Completion (WFC), and Food-Wine Pairing (FWP). SommBench is available in multiple languages: English, Slovak, Swedish, Finnish, German, Danish, Italian, and Spanish. This helps separate a language model's wine expertise from its language skills. The benchmark datasets were developed in close collaboration with a professional sommelier and native speakers of the respective languages, resulting in 1,024 wine theory question-answering questions, 1,000 wine feature-completion examples, and 1,000 food-wine pairing examples. We provide results for the most popular language models, including closed-weights models such as Gemini 2.5, and open-weights models, such as GPT-OSS and Qwen 3. Our results show that the most capable models perform well on wine theory question answering (up to 97% correct with a closed-weights model), yet feature completion (peaking at 65%) and food-wine pairing show (MCC ranging between 0 and 0.39) turn out to be more challenging. These results position SommBench as an interesting and challenging benchmark for evaluating the sommelier expertise of language models. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/sommify/sommbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SommBench：评估语言模型的品酒师专业能力
随着大语言模型的快速发展，系统评估其多语言和跨文化能力变得愈发重要。以往的文化评估基准主要关注可编码为语言形式的基本文化知识。本文提出SommBench，一个用于评估品酒师专业能力的多语言基准，该领域深深植根于嗅觉与味觉感官。尽管语言模型仅通过文本描述学习感官属性，SommBench旨在检验这种文本基础是否足以模拟专家级感官判断。SommBench包含三个主要任务：葡萄酒理论问答（WTQA）、葡萄酒特征补全（WFC）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（FWP）。该基准提供多种语言版本：英语、斯洛伐克语、瑞典语、芬兰语、德语、丹麦语、意大利语和西班牙语，有助于区分语言模型的葡萄酒专业知识与其语言能力。基准数据集在专业品酒师与各语言母语者紧密合作下开发，包含1024个葡萄酒理论问答题、1000个葡萄酒特征补全示例和1000个食物-葡萄酒搭配示例。我们提供了主流语言模型的结果，包括闭源模型如Gemini 2.5，以及开源模型如GPT-OSS和Qwen 3。结果显示，最强大的模型在葡萄酒理论问答中表现优异（闭源模型准确率高达97%），但在特征补全（最高65%）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（MCC介于0至0.39之间）任务中更具挑战性。这些结果使SommBench成为评估语言模型品酒师专业能力的一个有趣且具有挑战性的基准。该基准公开获取地址为https://github.com/sommify/sommbench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate language models' ability to emulate sommelier expertise beyond linguistic knowledge, this study introduces SommBench, a multilingual benchmark assessing sensory judgment in wine domains. The benchmark includes three tasks—Wine Theory Question Answering, Wine Feature Completion, and Food-Wine Pairing—developed with professional sommelier and native speaker collaboration across eight languages. Experimental results show that top models achieve near-perfect accuracy on wine theory questions (up to 97%), but significantly lower performance on feature completion (peak 65%) and food-wine pairing (MCC 0–0.39), indicating that textual grounding alone is insufficient for replicating expert sensory judgment.
为评估语言模型在超越语言知识层面模拟品酒师专业能力的能力，本研究提出SommBench多语言基准测试，涵盖葡萄酒理论问答、葡萄酒特征补全及食物配酒三个任务，由专业品酒师与母语者协作开发，覆盖八种语言。实验结果显示，顶尖模型在葡萄酒理论问答上准确率高达97%，但在特征补全（最高65%）和食物配酒（MCC 0–0.39）任务上表现显著下降，表明仅靠文本描述难以复现专家级感官判断能力。
Prompting Underestimates LLM Capability for Time Series Classification
Authors: Dan Schumacher, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Rocky Slavin, Anthony Rios
First: 2026-01-06T23:31:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:17:41+00:00
Comments: 8 pages + Appendix and References, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prompt-based evaluations suggest that large language models (LLMs) perform poorly on time series classification, raising doubts about whether they encode meaningful temporal structure. We show that this conclusion reflects limitations of prompt-based generation rather than the model's representational capacity by directly comparing prompt outputs with linear probes over the same internal representations. While zero-shot prompting performs near chance, linear probes improve average F1 from 0.15-0.26 to 0.61-0.67, often matching or exceeding specialized time series models. Layer-wise analyses further show that class-discriminative time series information emerges in early transformer layers and is amplified by visual and multimodal inputs. Together, these results demonstrate a systematic mismatch between what LLMs internally represent and what prompt-based evaluation reveals, leading current evaluations to underestimate their time series understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提示法低估了大语言模型在时间序列分类中的能力
基于提示的评估表明，大语言模型（LLMs）在时间序列分类任务中表现不佳，引发对其是否编码有意义时间结构的质疑。我们通过直接比较提示输出与线性探针在相同内部表示上的表现，表明这一结论反映的是提示生成的局限性，而非模型的表征能力。虽然零样本提示表现接近随机水平，但线性探针将平均F1分数从0.15-0.26提升至0.61-0.67，常与或超越专用时间序列模型相当。分层分析进一步表明，具有类区分能力的时间序列信息在Transformer早期层中出现，并被视觉和多模态输入放大。这些结果共同表明，LLMs内部表征与提示评估揭示的内容存在系统性不匹配，导致当前评估低估了其对时间序列的理解能力。
Summary / 总结
Prompt-based evaluations suggest that large language models (LLMs) perform poorly on time series classification, raising doubts about whether they encode meaningful temporal structure.
该论文探讨为何基于提示的评估认为大语言模型（LLM）在时间序列分类任务上表现不佳，质疑LLM是否真的缺乏时间结构理解。作者通过对比提示输出与应用于相同内部表示的线性探测器发现，零样本提示表现接近随机水平，而线性探测器的F1分数可达0.61–0.67，常与专用时间序列模型相当或更优。层间分析进一步表明，时间序列判别性信息在Transformer早期层即已出现，并在视觉或多模态输入下被增强，说明LLM内部确实编码了有意义的时间结构，但提示评估未能捕捉到，从而系统性低估了其能力。
Taming the Adversary: Stable Minimax Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient via Fractional Objectives
Authors: Taeho Lee, Donghwan Lee
First: 2026-03-12T16:15:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:15:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in a wide range of control and decision-making tasks. However, RL agents often exhibit unstable or degraded performance when deployed in environments subject to unexpected external disturbances and model uncertainties. Consequently, ensuring reliable performance under such conditions remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we propose minimax deep deterministic policy gradient (MMDDPG), a framework for learning disturbance-resilient policies in continuous control tasks. The training process is formulated as a minimax optimization problem between a user policy and an adversarial disturbance policy. In this problem, the user learns a robust policy that minimizes the objective function, while the adversary generates disturbances that maximize it. To stabilize this interaction, we introduce a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude. This objective prevents excessively aggressive disturbances and promotes robust learning. Experimental evaluations in MuJoCo environments demonstrate that the proposed MMDDPG achieves significantly improved robustness against both external force perturbations and model parameter variations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：驯服对手：通过分数目标实现稳定的最小最大深度确定性策略梯度
强化学习（RL）在广泛的控制与决策任务中取得了显著成功。然而，当RL代理部署在面临意外外部干扰和模型不确定性环境时，其性能往往不稳定或下降。因此，确保在这些条件下可靠性能仍是一个关键挑战。本文提出最小最大深度确定性策略梯度（MMDDPG），一种用于连续控制任务中学习抗干扰策略的框架。训练过程被建模为用户策略与对抗性干扰策略之间的最小最大优化问题。在此问题中，用户学习一个鲁棒策略以最小化目标函数，而对手生成最大化该函数的干扰。为稳定这种交互，我们引入一个分数目标，平衡任务性能与干扰幅度。该目标防止过度激进的干扰，并促进鲁棒学习。在MuJoCo环境中的实验评估表明，所提出的MMDDPG在对抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面显著提升了鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of reinforcement learning agents under external disturbances and model uncertainties, this paper introduces MMDDPG, a minimax framework that trains robust policies via adversarial interaction between a user policy and a disturbance policy. The method stabilizes learning by incorporating a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude, preventing overly aggressive adversarial actions. Experiments in MuJoCo environments show MMDDPG achieves significantly better robustness against external force perturbations and model parameter variations compared to baseline methods.
为解决强化学习代理在外部干扰和模型不确定性下性能不稳定的问题，本文提出MMDDPG框架，通过用户策略与对抗性干扰策略的最小-最大优化训练鲁棒策略。该方法引入分数目标函数，在任务性能与干扰强度间取得平衡，避免干扰过度激进，从而稳定学习过程。在MuJoCo环境中的实验表明，MMDDPG在抵抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面显著优于基线方法。
On Information Self-Locking in Reinforcement Learning for Active Reasoning of LLM agents
Authors: Deyu Zou, Yongqiang Chen, Fan Feng, Mufei Li, Pan Li, Yu Gong, James Cheng
First: 2026-03-12T16:14:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:14:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) with outcome-based rewards has achieved significant success in training large language model (LLM) agents for complex reasoning tasks. However, in active reasoning where agents need to strategically ask questions to acquire task-relevant information, we find that LLM agents trained with RL often suffer from information self-locking: the agent ceases to ask informative questions and struggles to internalize already-obtained information. To understand the phenomenon, we decompose active reasoning into two core capabilities: Action Selection (AS), which determines the observation stream through queries, and Belief Tracking (BT), which updates the agent's belief based on collected evidence. We show that deficient AS and BT capabilities will limit the information exploration during RL training. Furthermore, insufficient exploration in turn hinders the improvement of AS and BT, creating a feedback loop that locks the agent in a low-information regime. To resolve the issue, we propose a simple yet effective approach that reallocates the learning signal by injecting easy- to-obtain directional critiques to help the agent escape self-locking. Extensive experiments with 7 datasets show that our approach significantly mitigates the information self-locking, bringing up to 60% improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于强化学习中LLM代理主动推理的信息自锁现象
基于结果奖励的强化学习（RL）在训练大型语言模型（LLM）代理完成复杂推理任务方面取得了显著成功。然而，在主动推理场景中，代理需战略性提问以获取任务相关信息时，我们发现通过RL训练的LLM代理常出现信息自锁现象：代理停止提出有信息量的问题，且难以内化已获得的信息。为理解该现象，我们将主动推理分解为两个核心能力：动作选择（AS），通过提问决定观察流；信念追踪（BT），根据收集到的证据更新代理信念。我们表明，AS和BT能力不足会限制RL训练期间的信息探索。此外，探索不足反过来阻碍AS和BT能力的提升，形成反馈循环，使代理陷入低信息状态。为解决此问题，我们提出一种简单而有效的方法，通过注入易于获取的方向性批评，重新分配学习信号，帮助代理摆脱自锁。在7个数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著缓解了信息自锁，带来最高达60%的性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Reinforcement learning (RL) with outcome-based rewards has achieved significant success in training large language model (LLM) agents for complex reasoning tasks.
为了解决大语言模型在主动推理中因强化学习导致的信息自锁问题——即代理停止提问并难以内化已有信息——该研究将主动推理分解为动作选择与信念追踪两个核心能力，发现二者能力不足会形成限制探索的反馈循环。为此，作者提出一种通过注入易获取的定向批评来重新分配学习信号的方法，帮助代理突破自锁状态。在七个数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著缓解了信息自锁，最高提升效果达60%。
EvoTok: A Unified Image Tokenizer via Residual Latent Evolution for Visual Understanding and Generation
Authors: Yan Li, Ning Liao, Xiangyu Zhao, Shaofeng Zhang, Xiaoxing Wang, Yifan Yang, Junchi Yan, Xue Yang
First: 2026-03-12T16:13:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:13:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is fundamentally challenged by the granularity gap between visual understanding and generation: understanding requires high-level semantic abstractions, while image generation demands fine-grained pixel-level representations. Existing approaches usually enforce the two supervision on the same set of representation or decouple these two supervision on separate feature spaces, leading to interference and inconsistency, respectively. In this work, we propose EvoTok, a unified image tokenizer that reconciles these requirements through a residual evolution process within a shared latent space. Instead of maintaining separate token spaces for pixels and semantics, EvoTok encodes an image into a cascaded sequence of residual tokens via residual vector quantization. This residual sequence forms an evolution trajectory where earlier stages capture low-level details and deeper stages progressively transition toward high-level semantic representations. Despite being trained on a relatively modest dataset of 13M images, far smaller than the billion-scale datasets used by many previous unified tokenizers, EvoTok achieves a strong reconstruction quality of 0.43 rFID on ImageNet-1K at 256x256 resolution. When integrated with a large language model, EvoTok shows promising performance across 7 out of 9 visual understanding benchmarks, and remarkable results on image generation benchmarks such as GenEval and GenAI-Bench. These results demonstrate that modeling visual representations as an evolving trajectory provides an effective and principled solution for unifying visual understanding and generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoTok：通过残差潜在演化实现视觉理解与生成的统一图像标记器
统一多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的发展面临视觉理解与生成之间粒度差异的根本挑战：理解需要高层语义抽象，而图像生成则需要细粒度的像素级表示。现有方法通常强制在相同表示集上施加两种监督，或在不同特征空间中解耦这两种监督，分别导致干扰和不一致。本文提出EvoTok，一种通过共享潜在空间内残差演化过程协调这两种需求的统一图像标记器。EvoTok不维护像素与语义的独立标记空间，而是通过残差向量量化将图像编码为残差标记的级联序列。该残差序列构成演化轨迹，早期阶段捕捉低级细节，后期阶段逐步过渡至高层语义表示。尽管仅在1300万张图像的相对较小数据集上训练（远小于许多先前统一标记器所用的十亿级数据集），EvoTok在ImageNet-1K 256x256分辨率下仍实现0.43的rFID重建质量。当与大语言模型集成时，EvoTok在9个视觉理解基准中的7个上表现优异，并在GenEval和GenAI-Bench等图像生成基准上取得显著成果。这些结果表明，将视觉表示建模为演化轨迹，为统一视觉理解与生成提供了一种有效且原理清晰的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
The development of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is fundamentally challenged by the granularity gap between visual understanding and generation: understanding requires high-level semantic abstractions, while image generation demands fine-grained pixel-level representations.
To Words and Beyond: Probing Large Language Models for Sentence-Level Psycholinguistic Norms of Memorability and Reading Times
Authors: Thomas Hikaru Clark, Carlos Arriaga, Javier Conde, Gonzalo Martínez, Pedro Reviriego
First: 2026-03-12T16:10:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:10:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been shown to produce estimates of psycholinguistic norms, such as valence, arousal, or concreteness, for words and multiword expressions, that correlate with human judgments. These estimates are obtained by prompting an LLM, in zero-shot fashion, with a question similar to those used in human studies. Meanwhile, for other norms such as lexical decision time or age of acquisition, LLMs require supervised fine-tuning to obtain results that align with ground-truth values. In this paper, we extend this approach to the previously unstudied features of sentence memorability and reading times, which involve the relationship between multiple words in a sentence-level context. Our results show that via fine-tuning, models can provide estimates that correlate with human-derived norms and exceed the predictive power of interpretable baseline predictors, demonstrating that LLMs contain useful information about sentence-level features. At the same time, our results show very mixed zero-shot and few-shot performance, providing further evidence that care is needed when using LLM-prompting as a proxy for human cognitive measures.

标题：从词到句：探索大型语言模型在句子层面记忆性与阅读时间的心理语言学规范
大型语言模型（LLMs）近期被证明能生成词和多词表达的心理语言学规范估计值（如效价、唤醒度或具体性），这些估计值与人类判断相关。这些估计值通过零样本提示LLM获得，提示问题类似于人类研究中使用的问题。而对于其他规范（如词汇决策时间或习得年龄），LLMs需通过监督微调才能获得与真实值一致的结果。本文将此方法扩展至此前未被研究的句子层面特征——记忆性与阅读时间，这些特征涉及句子语境中多个词之间的关系。结果表明，通过微调，模型可提供与人类规范相关且超越可解释基线预测器预测能力的估计值，证明LLMs包含有关句子层面特征的有用信息。同时，我们的结果也显示零样本和少量样本表现非常不一致，进一步表明在使用LLM提示作为人类认知测量代理时需谨慎。
