Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been shown to produce estimates of psycholinguistic norms, such as valence, arousal, or concreteness, for words and multiword expressions, that correlate with human judgments.
鉴于大语言模型在估计词级心理语言学规范方面的能力，本研究探索其是否也能预测句子级的记忆性和阅读时间——此前未被研究的句子层面特征。作者对LLM进行微调，使其预测人类标注的这些句子级规范，并与可解释基线模型进行比较。结果表明，微调后的模型与人类判断高度相关，且优于基线预测器，说明LLM编码了有用的句子级认知信息。然而，零样本和少样本提示表现不稳定，表明在未监督情况下，LLM难以可靠替代人类认知测量。
Whleaper: A 10-DOF Flexible Bipedal Wheeled Robot
Authors: Yinglei Zhu, Sixiao He, Yan Ning, Zhenghao Qi, Zhuoyuan Yong, Yihua Qin, Jianyu Chen
Venue: IROS
First: 2025-04-30T16:07:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:09:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wheel-legged robots combine the advantages of both wheeled robots and legged robots, offering versatile locomotion capabilities with excellent stability on challenging terrains and high efficiency on flat surfaces. However, existing wheel-legged robots typically have limited hip joint mobility compared to humans, while hip joint plays a crucial role in locomotion. In this paper, we introduce Whleaper, a novel 10-degree-of-freedom (DOF) bipedal wheeled robot, with 3 DOFs at the hip of each leg. Its humanoid joint design enables adaptable motion in complex scenarios, ensuring stability and flexibility. This paper introduces the details of Whleaper, with a focus on innovative mechanical design, control algorithms and system implementation. Firstly, stability stems from the increased DOFs at the hip, which expand the range of possible postures and improve the robot's foot-ground contact. Secondly, the extra DOFs also augment its mobility. During walking or sliding, more complex movements can be adopted to execute obstacle avoidance tasks. Thirdly, we utilize two control algorithms to implement multimodal motion for walking and sliding. By controlling specific DOFs of the robot, we conducted a series of simulations and practical experiments, demonstrating that a high-DOF hip joint design can effectively enhance the stability and flexibility of wheel-legged robots. Whleaper shows its capability to perform actions such as squatting, obstacle avoidance sliding, and rapid turning in real-world scenarios.

Wheel-legged robots combine the advantages of both wheeled robots and legged robots, offering versatile locomotion capabilities with excellent stability on challenging terrains and high efficiency on flat surfaces.
FedSKD: Aggregation-free Model-heterogeneous Federated Learning via Multi-dimensional Similarity Knowledge Distillation for Medical Image Classification
Authors: Ziqiao Weng, Weidong Cai, Bo Zhou
First: 2025-03-23T05:33:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:08:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE-TNNLS, 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training without direct data sharing. Model-heterogeneous FL (MHFL) extends this paradigm by allowing clients to train personalized models with heterogeneous architectures tailored to their computational resources and application-specific needs. However, existing MHFL methods predominantly rely on centralized aggregation, which introduces scalability and efficiency bottlenecks, or impose restrictions requiring partially identical model architectures across clients. While peer-to-peer (P2P) FL removes server dependence, it suffers from model drift and knowledge dilution, limiting its effectiveness in heterogeneous settings. To address these challenges, we propose FedSKD, a novel MHFL framework that facilitates direct knowledge exchange through round-robin model circulation, eliminating the need for centralized aggregation while allowing fully heterogeneous model architectures across clients. FedSKD's key innovation lies in multi-dimensional similarity knowledge distillation, which enables bidirectional cross-client knowledge transfer at batch, pixel/voxel, and region levels for heterogeneous models in FL. This approach mitigates catastrophic forgetting and model drift through progressive reinforcement and distribution alignment while preserving model heterogeneity. Extensive evaluations on fMRI-based autism spectrum disorder diagnosis and skin lesion classification demonstrate that FedSKD outperforms state-of-the-art heterogeneous and homogeneous FL baselines, achieving superior personalization (client-specific accuracy) and generalization (cross-institutional adaptability). These findings underscore FedSKD's potential as a scalable and robust solution for real-world medical federated learning applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedSKD：通过多维相似性知识蒸馏实现无聚合的模型异构联邦学习用于医学图像分类
联邦学习（FL）可在不直接共享数据的前提下实现隐私保护的协同模型训练。模型异构联邦学习（MHFL）扩展了这一范式，允许客户端根据其计算资源和应用需求训练个性化模型，采用异构架构。然而，现有MHFL方法主要依赖集中式聚合，导致可扩展性和效率瓶颈，或强制要求客户端模型架构部分相同。虽然点对点（P2P）FL消除了服务器依赖，但其易受模型漂移和知识稀释影响，限制了其在异构环境中的有效性。为解决这些问题，我们提出FedSKD，一种新颖的MHFL框架，通过轮询模型循环实现直接知识交换，无需集中式聚合，同时允许客户端采用完全异构的模型架构。FedSKD的核心创新在于多维相似性知识蒸馏，可在批次、像素/体素和区域层面实现异构模型间的双向跨客户端知识传递，通过渐进强化和分布对齐缓解灾难性遗忘和模型漂移，同时保留模型异构性。在基于fMRI的自闭症谱系障碍诊断和皮肤病变分类上的广泛评估表明，FedSKD优于当前最先进的异构和同构FL基线方法，在个性化（客户端特定准确率）和泛化能力（跨机构适应性）方面表现更优。这些结果凸显了FedSKD作为可扩展且稳健的现实世界医学联邦学习解决方案的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, privacy-preserving federated learning in medical imaging with heterogeneous client models, FedSKD introduces an aggregation-free framework that enables direct peer-to-peer knowledge exchange via round-robin model circulation. Its core method employs multi-dimensional similarity knowledge distillation, facilitating bidirectional knowledge transfer across clients at batch, pixel/voxel, and region levels to align distributions and prevent model drift. Experimental results on autism spectrum disorder diagnosis and skin lesion classification show FedSKD achieves higher client-specific accuracy and cross-institutional generalization than existing heterogeneous and homogeneous FL baselines, validating its effectiveness in real-world medical applications.
为解决模型异构联邦学习中中心化聚合的瓶颈和模型架构刚性限制，FedSKD提出一种无需聚合的框架，支持客户端间直接知识交换，允许完全异构模型架构。其核心创新是多维相似性知识蒸馏，通过批次、像素/体素和区域层级实现双向知识传递，缓解模型漂移与灾难性遗忘，同时保持架构多样性。在医学图像分类任务（自闭症谱系障碍诊断与皮肤病变分类）上的实验表明，FedSKD在客户端特异性准确率和跨机构泛化能力上均优于现有异构与同构联邦学习方法。
Wasserstein Gradient Flows for Batch Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design
Authors: Louis Sharrock
First: 2026-03-12T16:08:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:08:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian optimal experimental design (BOED) provides a powerful, decision-theoretic framework for selecting experiments so as to maximise the expected utility of the data to be collected. In practice, however, its applicability can be limited by the difficulty of optimising the chosen utility. The expected information gain (EIG), for example, is often high-dimensional and strongly non-convex. This challenge is particularly acute in the batch setting, where multiple experiments are to be designed simultaneously. In this paper, we introduce a new approach to batch EIG-based BOED via a probabilistic lifting of the original optimisation problem to the space of probability measures. In particular, we propose to optimise an entropic regularisation of the expected utility over the space of design measures. Under mild conditions, we show that this objective admits a unique minimiser, which can be explicitly characterised in the form of a Gibbs distribution. The resulting design law can be used directly as a randomised batch-design policy, or as a computational relaxation from which a deterministic batch is extracted. To obtain scalable approximations when the batch size is large, we then consider two tractable restrictions of the full batch distribution: a mean-field family, and an i.i.d. product family. For the i.i.d. objective, and formally for its mean-field extension, we derive the corresponding Wasserstein gradient flow, characterise its long-time behaviour, and obtain particle-based algorithms via space-time discretisations. We also introduce doubly stochastic variants that combine interacting particle updates with Monte Carlo estimators of the EIG gradient. Finally, we illustrate the performance of the proposed methods in several numerical experiments, demonstrating their ability to explore multimodal optimisation landscapes and obtain high-utility batches in challenging examples.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Wasserstein梯度流的批量贝叶斯最优实验设计
贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）提供了一个强大的决策理论框架，用于选择实验以最大化所收集数据的预期效用。然而，在实践中，由于所选效用的优化难度，其适用性可能受限。例如，预期信息增益（EIG）往往是高维且强非凸的。这一挑战在批量设置中尤为突出，即需同时设计多个实验。本文提出了一种新的基于批量EIG的BOED方法，通过将原始优化问题概率提升至概率测度空间。具体而言，我们提出在设计测度空间上优化预期效用的熵正则化形式。在温和条件下，我们证明该目标函数存在唯一最小值，可显式表征为Gibbs分布。所得设计策略可直接作为随机批量设计策略，或作为计算松弛方案，从中提取确定性批量。为在批量规模较大时获得可扩展近似，我们考虑了完整批量分布的两个可处理限制：均场族和独立同分布（i.i.d.）乘积族。对于i.i.d.目标及其均场扩展，我们推导了相应的Wasserstein梯度流，刻画其长期行为，并通过时空离散化获得粒子算法。此外，我们引入了双重随机变体，结合粒子交互更新与EIG梯度的蒙特卡洛估计。最后，我们在多个数值实验中展示了所提方法的性能，证明其在复杂示例中能够探索多模态优化景观并获得高效用批量。
Summary / 总结
Bayesian optimal experimental design (BOED) provides a powerful, decision-theoretic framework for selecting experiments so as to maximise the expected utility of the data to be collected.
为解决批量贝叶斯最优实验设计中高维非凸预期信息增益的优化难题，本文提出将设计问题概率化提升至测度空间，并通过熵正则化实现可计算优化，其目标函数存在唯一吉布斯形式最小值，可直接作为随机批量策略或松弛为确定性设计。为应对大规模批量场景，作者引入均值场与独立同分布测度族，推导其对应的Wasserstein梯度流并设计粒子算法，结合蒙特卡洛梯度估计实现双随机更新。实验表明，该方法能有效探索多峰优化空间并生成高价值实验批量。
Towards Dynamic Model Identification and Gravity Compensation for the dVRK-Si Patient Side Manipulator
Authors: Haoying Zhou, Hao Yang, Brendan Burkhart, Anton Deguet, Loris Fichera, Gregory S. Fischer, Jie Ying Wu, Peter Kazanzides
First: 2026-03-12T16:06:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:06:16+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Medical Robotics and Bionics (T-MRB), under review. Open-source GitHub Repo: https://github.com/jhu-dvrk/dvrk_psm_dynamics_identification
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) is widely used for research in robot-assisted surgery, but most modeling and control methods target the first-generation dVRK Classic. The recently introduced dVRK-Si, built from da Vinci Si hardware, features a redesigned Patient Side Manipulator (PSM) with substantially larger gravity loading, which can degrade control if unmodeled. This paper presents the first complete kinematic and dynamic modeling framework for the dVRK-Si PSM. We derive a modified DH kinematic model that captures the closed-chain parallelogram mechanism, formulate dynamics via the Euler-Lagrange method, and express inverse dynamics in a linear-in-parameters regressor form. Dynamic parameters are identified from data collected on a periodic excitation trajectory optimized for numerical conditioning and estimated by convex optimization with physical feasibility constraints. Using the identified model, we implement real-time gravity compensation and computed-torque feedforward in the dVRK control stack. Experiments on a physical dVRK-Si show that the gravity compensation reduces steady-state joint errors by 68-84% and decreases end-effector tip drift during static holds from 4.2 mm to 0.7 mm. Computed-torque feedforward further improves transient and position tracking accuracy. For sinusoidal trajectory tracking, computed-torque feedforward reduces position errors by 35% versus gravity-only feedforward and by 40% versus PID-only. The proposed pipeline supports reliable control, high-fidelity simulation, and learning-based automation on the dVRK-Si.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向dVRK-Si患者侧操作臂的动态模型辨识与重力补偿研究
达芬奇研究套件（dVRK）广泛用于机器人辅助手术研究，但多数建模与控制方法针对第一代dVRK Classic。新近推出的dVRK-Si基于达芬奇Si硬件，其患者侧操作臂（PSM）重新设计，重力负载显著增大，若未建模将影响控制性能。本文首次提出dVRK-Si PSM完整的运动学与动力学建模框架：推导改进的DH运动学模型以表征闭链平行四边形机构，通过欧拉-拉格朗日方法建立动力学方程，并将逆动力学表达为参数线性回归形式。动态参数通过优化数值条件的周期激励轨迹采集数据，并结合物理可行性约束的凸优化方法进行辨识。基于辨识模型，我们在dVRK控制栈中实现实时重力补偿与计算扭矩前馈控制。在物理dVRK-Si实验中，重力补偿使关节稳态误差降低68-84%，静态保持时末端执行器漂移从4.2mm降至0.7mm。计算扭矩前馈进一步提升瞬态响应与位置跟踪精度：在正弦轨迹跟踪中，相比仅重力补偿前馈，位置误差降低35%；相比仅PID控制，降低40%。该流程支持dVRK-Si的可靠控制、高保真仿真与基于学习的自动化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate dynamic modeling of the newer dVRK-Si surgical robot to handle its increased gravity loads, this paper develops a complete kinematic and dynamic framework tailored to its redesigned Patient Side Manipulator. The method employs a modified Denavit-Hartenberg model to capture its closed-chain parallelogram mechanism, derives dynamics via Euler-Lagrange equations, and formulates inverse dynamics as a linear-in-parameters regressor. Dynamic parameters are identified using convex optimization with physical constraints from data collected on a specially designed periodic trajectory. Experimental results on a physical dVRK-Si show that gravity compensation reduces joint steady-state errors by 68–84% and end-effector drift from 4.2 mm to 0.7 mm during static holds. Computed-torque feedforward further improves trajectory tracking, reducing position errors by 35% compared to gravity-only and 40% compared to PID-only control.
为解决新型dVRK-Si患者侧机械臂因重力负载增大导致控制性能下降的问题，本文构建了完整的运动学与动力学建模框架。通过改进Denavit-Hartenberg模型描述闭链平行四边形机构，采用拉格朗日方法推导动力学方程，并将逆动力学表示为参数线性回归形式。利用优化设计的周期激励轨迹采集数据，通过凸优化结合物理可行性约束识别动力学参数，并应用于实时重力补偿与计算扭矩前馈控制。实验表明，重力补偿使关节稳态误差降低68–84%，末端漂移从4.2毫米降至0.7毫米；计算扭矩前馈进一步使正弦轨迹跟踪误差较重力补偿单独使用降低35%，较PID控制降低40%，实现可靠控制与高保真仿真。
A Robust and Efficient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Traffic Signal Control
Authors: Sheng-You Huang, Hsiao-Chuan Chang, Yen-Chi Chen, Ting-Han Wei, I-Hau Yeh, Sheng-Yao Kuan, Chien-Yao Wang, Hsuan-Han Lee, I-Chen Wu
First: 2026-03-12T16:02:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:02:28+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 tables, 8 figures. Under review in the 31st ITS World Congress 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Traffic Signal Control (TSC) faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations. This paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework validated in the Vissim traffic simulator. The framework integrates three mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization, a training strategy that exposes agents to dynamic turning probabilities to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, which balances responsiveness and precision through cyclical, exponential phase adjustments; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme utilizing the MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE). By leveraging centralized updates, this approach approximates the efficacy of global observations while maintaining scalable local communication. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability, offering a practical solution for adaptive signal control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向交通信号控制的鲁棒高效多智能体强化学习框架
强化学习（RL）在交通信号控制（TSC）中的实际部署面临显著挑战，主要源于其对动态交通流变化的泛化能力有限。现有方法常过度拟合静态模式，且动作空间与驾驶员预期不兼容。本文提出一种经Vissim交通仿真器验证的鲁棒多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，集成三项机制：（1）转向比例随机化训练策略，通过动态转向概率增强对未见场景的鲁棒性；（2）以稳定性为导向的指数相位持续时间调整动作空间，通过循环指数相位调整平衡响应性与精度；（3）基于邻居的观测方案，采用MAPPO算法结合集中训练与分散执行（CTDE）。该方法通过集中更新近似全局观测效果，同时保持可扩展的局部通信。实验表明，本框架优于标准RL基线，平均等待时间降低超10%，在未见交通场景中泛化能力更强，控制稳定性高，为自适应信号控制提供实用方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization and action-space mismatch in existing reinforcement learning approaches for traffic signal control, this paper introduces a robust multi-agent reinforcement learning framework validated in the Vissim simulator. The method combines turning ratio randomization to simulate dynamic traffic patterns, an exponential phase duration adjustment for stable yet responsive signal timing, and a neighbor-based observation scheme using MAPPO with centralized training and decentralized execution. Experiments show the framework reduces average waiting time by over 10% compared to standard baselines and demonstrates strong generalization and stability in unseen traffic conditions, offering a practical solution for adaptive traffic signal management.
为解决现有强化学习方法在交通信号控制中泛化能力差、动作空间与驾驶员预期不匹配的问题，本文提出一种鲁棒的多智能体强化学习框架，并在Vissim仿真环境中验证。该方法结合转向比例随机化以模拟动态交通流、指数相位持续时间调整以实现稳定且响应迅速的信号控制，以及基于邻居的观测方案，采用MAPPO算法配合集中训练与分散执行。实验表明，该框架相较标准基线平均等待时间降低超10%，在未见交通场景中泛化能力与稳定性表现更优，为自适应交通信号控制提供了实用方案。
Human-Centred LLM Privacy Audits: Findings and Frictions
Authors: Dimitri Staufer, Kirsten Morehouse, David Hartmann, Bettina Berendt
First: 2026-03-12T16:01:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:01:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) learn statistical associations from massive training corpora and user interactions, and deployed systems can surface or infer information about individuals. Yet people lack practical ways to inspect what a model associates with their name. We report interim findings from an ongoing study and introduce LMP2, a browser-based self-audit tool. In two user studies ($N_{total}{=}458$), GPT-4o predicts 11 of 50 features for everyday people with $\ge$60\% accuracy, and participants report wanting control over LLM-generated associations despite not considering all outputs privacy violations. To validate our probing method, we evaluate eight LLMs on public figures and non-existent names, observing clear separation between stable name-conditioned associations and model defaults. Our findings also contribute to exposing a broader generative AI evaluation crisis: when outputs are probabilistic, context-dependent, and user-mediated through elicitation, what model--individual associations even include is under-specified and operationalisation relies on crafting probes and metrics that are hard to validate or compare. To move towards reliable, actionable human-centred LLM privacy audits, we identify nine frictions that emerged in our study and offer recommendations for future work and the design of human-centred LLM privacy audits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以人为中心的LLM隐私审计：发现与摩擦
大型语言模型（LLMs）从大规模训练语料和用户交互中学习统计关联，部署系统可能揭示或推断个人相关信息。然而，人们缺乏实际手段检查模型对其姓名的关联内容。我们报告了正在进行研究的阶段性发现，并引入了LMP2，一款基于浏览器的自我审计工具。在两项用户研究（总样本量N=458）中，GPT-4o对普通人的50个特征预测准确率≥60%，参与者虽未认为所有输出均为隐私违规，但仍希望控制LLM生成的关联。为验证我们的探测方法，我们在公众人物和虚构姓名上评估了八种LLM，观察到稳定姓名条件关联与模型默认值之间存在明显区分。我们的发现也揭示了生成式AI评估危机：当输出具有概率性、上下文依赖性，并通过引导用户交互时，模型个体关联内容的定义不明确，操作化依赖于难以验证或比较的探测与指标设计。为推动可靠、可操作的人本导向LLM隐私审计，我们识别出研究中出现的九项摩擦，并提出未来工作及人本导向LLM隐私审计设计的建议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of practical tools for users to inspect how large language models (LLMs) associate personal names with sensitive features, the study introduces LMP2, a browser-based self-audit tool. Through two user studies involving 458 participants, GPT-4o was found to accurately predict 11 out of 50 personal attributes for everyday individuals with at least 60% accuracy, while users expressed desire for control over model-generated associations despite not perceiving all outputs as privacy violations. The researchers validated their probing method by testing eight LLMs on public figures and fabricated names, revealing clear distinctions between stable name-conditioned associations and default model outputs. The study also highlights a broader crisis in generative AI evaluation: probabilistic, context-dependent outputs and user-mediated elicitation make it difficult to define and measure individual model associations, necessitating new probe design and validation frameworks. Nine key frictions in human-centred privacy audits are identified, offering guidance for future research and tool design.
为解决用户缺乏工具检查LLM如何将个人姓名与敏感特征关联的问题，研究提出了基于浏览器的自审计工具LMP2，并进行了两项包含458名参与者的用户研究。结果显示，GPT-4o对日常用户50个特征中的11个预测准确率至少达60%，而参与者虽未将所有输出视为隐私违规，仍希望对模型生成的关联拥有控制权。研究通过测试八种LLM在公众人物和虚构姓名上的表现，验证了其探针方法，发现稳定姓名条件关联与模型默认输出存在明显区分。同时，研究识别出九项阻碍可靠人本化LLM隐私审计的摩擦点，强调在概率性、上下文依赖的生成式AI评估中，需改进探针设计与可验证的评估指标。
Resource-Efficient Iterative LLM-Based NAS with Feedback Memory
Authors: Xiaojie Gu, Dmitry Ignatov, Radu Timofte
First: 2026-03-12T16:00:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:00:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural Architecture Search (NAS) automates network design, but conventional methods demand substantial computational resources. We propose a closed-loop pipeline leveraging large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate, evaluate, and refine convolutional neural network architectures for image classification on a single consumer-grade GPU without LLM fine-tuning. Central to our approach is a historical feedback memory inspired by Markov chains: a sliding window of $K{=}5$ recent improvement attempts keeps context size constant while providing sufficient signal for iterative learning. Unlike prior LLM optimizers that discard failure trajectories, each history entry is a structured diagnostic triple -- recording the identified problem, suggested modification, and resulting outcome -- treating code execution failures as first-class learning signals. A dual-LLM specialization reduces per-call cognitive load: a Code Generator produces executable PyTorch architectures while a Prompt Improver handles diagnostic reasoning. Since both the LLM and architecture training share limited VRAM, the search implicitly favors compact, hardware-efficient models suited to edge deployment. We evaluate three frozen instruction-tuned LLMs (${\leq}7$B parameters) across up to 2000 iterations in an unconstrained open code space, using one-epoch proxy accuracy on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette as a fast ranking signal. On CIFAR-10, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B improves from 28.2% to 69.2%, Qwen2.5-7B from 50.0% to 71.5%, and GLM-5 from 43.2% to 62.0%. A full 2000-iteration search completes in ${\approx}18$ GPU hours on a single RTX~4090, establishing a low-budget, reproducible, and hardware-aware paradigm for LLM-driven NAS without cloud infrastructure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于反馈记忆的资源高效迭代式LLM驱动神经架构搜索
神经架构搜索（NAS）可自动化网络设计，但传统方法需大量计算资源。我们提出一种闭环流程，利用大语言模型（LLM）在单张消费级GPU上无需微调，迭代生成、评估和优化用于图像分类的卷积神经网络架构。核心是受马尔可夫链启发的历史反馈记忆：一个包含5次最近改进尝试的滑动窗口，保持上下文大小恒定，同时提供足够的迭代学习信号。与先前LLM优化器丢弃失败轨迹不同，每个历史条目均为结构化诊断三元组——记录识别的问题、建议修改及结果——将代码执行失败视为一等学习信号。双LLM专业化降低每次调用的认知负载：代码生成器生成可执行PyTorch架构，提示优化器处理诊断推理。由于LLM与架构训练共享有限VRAM，搜索隐式偏向紧凑、硬件高效的模型，适合边缘部署。我们在无约束开放代码空间中，对三款冻结指令调优的LLM（≤7B参数）进行最多2000次迭代评估，使用CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNette的一轮代理准确率作为快速排序信号。在CIFAR-10上，DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B准确率从28.2%提升至69.2%，Qwen2.5-7B从50.0%提升至71.5%，GLM-5从43.2%提升至62.0%。完整2000次迭代搜索在单张RTX 4090上约需18GPU小时，建立了一种无需云基础设施、低成本、可复现且硬件感知的LLM驱动NAS范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of traditional Neural Architecture Search (NAS), this work proposes a resource-efficient iterative NAS pipeline using large language models (LLMs) on a single consumer GPU without fine-tuning. The method employs a feedback memory based on Markov chains, maintaining a sliding window of five recent architecture attempts as structured diagnostic triples to guide iterative refinement. Two specialized LLMs — one for generating executable PyTorch code and another for improving prompts — reduce per-call load while implicitly favoring compact, VRAM-efficient models. Evaluated across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette using one-epoch proxy accuracy, the approach achieves significant performance gains: DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B improves from 28.2% to 69.2% on CIFAR-10, Qwen2.5-7B from 50.0% to 71.5%, and GLM-5 from 43.2% to 62.0%. A full 2000-iteration search completes in approximately 18 GPU hours on an RTX 4090, demonstrating a scalable, low-budget, and hardware-aware NAS paradigm.
为降低传统神经架构搜索（NAS）的高计算开销，该研究提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的闭环迭代方法，可在单张消费级显卡上无需微调地自主设计卷积神经网络。方法采用基于马尔可夫链的历史反馈记忆机制，保留最近五次架构尝试作为结构化诊断三元组，将失败视为重要学习信号以指导迭代优化。通过双LLM分工——一个生成可执行PyTorch代码，另一个负责诊断提示优化——降低每次调用的认知负担并适配显存受限硬件。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNette上使用单轮代理精度评估，结果显著提升：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B从28.2%提升至69.2%，Qwen2.5-7B从50.0%提升至71.5%，GLM-5从43.2%提升至62.0%，完整2000次迭代仅需约18小时RTX 4090显卡时间。
